```
37	class ExecutorRegistryError(ExecutorValidationError):
38	    """Raised when a executor registry is inconsistent."""
39	
40	
41	class ExecutorRegistry:
42	    """Small in-memory registry keyed by executor id."""
43	
44	    def __init__(self, executors: Iterable[ExecutorDefinition | dict[str, Any]] = ()) -> None:
45	        self._executors: dict[str, ExecutorDefinition] = {}
46	        for executor in executors:
47	            self.register(executor)
48	
49	    def register(self, executor: ExecutorDefinition | dict[str, Any]) -> ExecutorDefinition:
50	        definition = validate_executor_definition(executor)
51	        if definition.id in self._executors:
52	            raise ExecutorRegistryError(f"duplicate executor id {definition.id!r}")
53	        self._executors[definition.id] = definition
54	        return definition
55	
56	    def get(self, executor_id: str) -> ExecutorDefinition:
57	        try:
58	            return self._executors[executor_id]
59	        except KeyError as exc:
60	            raise KeyError(f"unknown executor id {executor_id!r}") from exc
61	
62	    def list(self, kind: str | None = None) -> tuple[ExecutorDefinition, ...]:
63	        if kind is not None and kind not in {"built_in", "external"}:
64	            raise ExecutorRegistryError("kind must be one of ['built_in', 'external']")
65	        executors = self._executors.values()
66	        if kind is not None:
67	            executors = [executor for executor in executors if executor.kind == kind]
68	        return tuple(sorted(executors, key=lambda executor: executor.id))
69	
70	    def validate_all(self) -> tuple[ExecutorDefinition, ...]:
71	        for executor in self._executors.values():
72	            validate_executor_definition(executor)
73	        self._validate_graph_references()
74	        return self.list()
75	
76	    def to_dict(self, kind: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
77	        return {"executors": [executor.to_dict() for executor in self.list(kind=kind)]}
78	
79	    def to_json(self, *, kind: str | None = None, indent: int | None = 2) -> str:
80	        return json.dumps(self.to_dict(kind=kind), indent=indent, sort_keys=True)
81	
82	    def as_mapping(self) -> MappingProxyType[str, ExecutorDefinition]:
83	        return MappingProxyType(dict(self._executors))
84	
85	    def _validate_graph_references(self) -> None:
86	        known_ids = set(self._executors)
87	        for executor in self._executors.values():
88	            for dependency in executor.graph.depends_on:
89	                if dependency not in known_ids:
90	                    raise ExecutorRegistryError(f"executor {executor.id!r} depends on unknown executor {dependency!r}")
91	                if dependency == executor.id:
92	                    raise ExecutorRegistryError(f"executor {executor.id!r} cannot depend on itself")
93	
94	
95	def executor_id_for_step(step_name: str) -> str:
96	    return f"builtin.{step_name}"
97	
98	
99	def load_builtin_executors() -> tuple[ExecutorDefinition, ...]:
100	    folder_executors: list[ExecutorDefinition] = []
101	    expected_ids = {executor_id_for_step(step_name) for step_name in BUILTIN_STEP_ORDER}
102	    for path in _builtin_folder_roots():
103	        folder_executors.extend(load_folder_executors(path))
104	    by_id = {executor.id: executor for executor in folder_executors if executor.id in expected_ids}
105	    missing = [executor_id_for_step(step_name) for step_name in BUILTIN_STEP_ORDER if executor_id_for_step(step_name) not in by_id]
106	    if missing:
107	        raise ExecutorRegistryError(f"missing built-in executor folder metadata: {', '.join(missing)}")
108	    return tuple(by_id[executor_id_for_step(step_name)] for step_name in BUILTIN_STEP_ORDER)
109	
110	
111	def load_curated_executors() -> tuple[ExecutorDefinition, ...]:
112	    executors: list[ExecutorDefinition] = []
113	    for path in _curated_manifest_paths():
114	        executors.append(load_executor_manifest(path))
115	    for path in _curated_folder_roots():
116	        executors.extend(load_folder_executors(path))
117	    return tuple(executors)
118

123	        executors.append(load_executor_manifest(path))
124	    for path in _bundled_folder_roots():

129	def load_default_registry(banodoco_config: BanodocoCatalogConfig | None = None) -> ExecutorRegistry:
130	    registry = ExecutorRegistry()
131	    for executor in load_builtin_executors():
132	        registry.register(executor)
133	    for executor in load_bundled_executors():
134	        registry.register(executor)
135	    for executor in load_curated_executors():
136	        registry.register(executor)
137	    for executor in load_project_executors():
138	        if executor.id not in registry.as_mapping():
139	            registry.register(executor)
140	    if banodoco_config is not None and banodoco_config.enabled:
141	        for executor in load_banodoco_catalog_executors(banodoco_config):
142	            registry.register(executor)
143	    registry.validate_all()
144	    return registry
145

148	    executors: list[ExecutorDefinition] = []

151	    return tuple(executors)

155	    return _package_manifest_paths(CURATED_PACKAGE, Path(__file__).with_name("curated"))
156	
157	
158	def _curated_folder_roots() -> tuple[Path, ...]:
159	    return _package_folder_roots(CURATED_PACKAGE, Path(__file__).with_name("curated"))
160	
161	
162	def _bundled_manifest_paths() -> tuple[Path, ...]:
163	    return _package_manifest_paths(BUNDLED_PACKAGE, Path(__file__).parents[1] / "executors" / "bundled")
164	
165	
166	def _bundled_folder_roots() -> tuple[Path, ...]:
167	    return _package_folder_roots(BUNDLED_PACKAGE, Path(__file__).parents[1] / "executors" / "bundled")
168	
169	
170	def _builtin_folder_roots() -> tuple[Path, ...]:
171	    source_root = Path(__file__).parent
172	    return tuple(source_root / step_name for step_name in BUILTIN_STEP_ORDER if (source_root / step_name).is_dir())
173	
174	
175	def _project_folder_roots() -> tuple[Path, ...]:
176	    source_root = Path(__file__).parent
177	    skipped = {"__pycache__", "actions", "builtin", "bundled", "curated"}
178	    if not source_root.is_dir():
179	        return ()
180	    return tuple(
181	        sorted(
182	            path
183	            for path in source_root.iterdir()
184	            if path.is_dir() and path.name not in skipped and not path.name.startswith(".")
185	        )
186	    )
187	
188	
189	def _package_manifest_paths(package: str, source_root: Path) -> tuple[Path, ...]:
190	    paths: list[Path] = []
191	    try:
192	        package_root = resources.files(package)
193	        paths.extend(Path(str(item)) for item in package_root.iterdir() if item.name.endswith(".json"))
194	    except (FileNotFoundError, ModuleNotFoundError, TypeError):
195	        pass
196	
197	    if paths:
198	        return tuple(sorted(paths))
199	
200	    if not source_root.is_dir():
201	        return ()
202	    return tuple(sorted(source_root.glob("*.json")))
203	
204	
205	def _package_folder_roots(package: str, source_root: Path) -> tuple[Path, ...]:
206	    paths: list[Path] = []
207	    try:
208	        package_root = resources.files(package)
209	        paths.extend(Path(str(item)) for item in package_root.iterdir() if item.is_dir())
210	    except (FileNotFoundError, ModuleNotFoundError, TypeError):
211	        pass
212	
213	    if paths:
214	        return tuple(sorted(paths))
215	
216	    if not source_root.is_dir():
217	        return ()
218	    return tuple(sorted(path for path in source_root.iterdir() if path.is_dir()))
219
```

```
1	"""Registry and discovery helpers for ArtAgents orchestrators."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import json
6	from importlib import resources
7	from pathlib import Path
8	from types import MappingProxyType
9	from typing import Any, Iterable
10	
11	from artagents.executors.registry import ExecutorRegistry, load_default_registry as load_default_executor_registry
12	
13	from .schema import (
14	    OrchestratorDefinition,
15	    OrchestratorValidationError,
16	    load_orchestrator_manifest,
17	    validate_orchestrator_definition,
18	)
19	from .folder import load_folder_orchestrators
20	
21	
22	BUNDLED_PACKAGE = "artagents.orchestrators.bundled"
23	CURATED_PACKAGE = "artagents.orchestrators.curated"
24	
25	
26	class OrchestratorRegistryError(OrchestratorValidationError):
27	    """Raised when an orchestrator registry is inconsistent."""
28	
29	
30	class OrchestratorRegistry:
31	    """Small in-memory registry keyed by orchestrator id."""
32	
33	    def __init__(
34	        self,
35	        orchestrators: Iterable[OrchestratorDefinition | dict[str, Any]] = (),
36	        *,
37	        executor_registry: ExecutorRegistry | None = None,
38	    ) -> None:
39	        self._orchestrators: dict[str, OrchestratorDefinition] = {}
40	        self._executor_registry = executor_registry
41	        for orchestrator in orchestrators:
42	            self.register(orchestrator)
43	
44	    def register(self, orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition | dict[str, Any]) -> OrchestratorDefinition:
45	        definition = validate_orchestrator_definition(orchestrator)
46	        if definition.id in self._orchestrators:
47	            raise OrchestratorRegistryError(f"duplicate orchestrator id {definition.id!r}")
48	        self._orchestrators[definition.id] = definition
49	        return definition
50	
51	    def get(self, orchestrator_id: str) -> OrchestratorDefinition:
52	        try:
53	            return self._orchestrators[orchestrator_id]
54	        except KeyError as exc:
55	            raise KeyError(f"unknown orchestrator id {orchestrator_id!r}") from exc
56	
57	    def list(self, kind: str | None = None) -> tuple[OrchestratorDefinition, ...]:
58	        if kind is not None and kind not in {"built_in", "external"}:
59	            raise OrchestratorRegistryError("kind must be one of ['built_in', 'external']")
60	        orchestrators: Iterable[OrchestratorDefinition] = self._orchestrators.values()
61	        if kind is not None:
62	            orchestrators = [orchestrator for orchestrator in orchestrators if orchestrator.kind == kind]
63	        return tuple(sorted(orchestrators, key=lambda orchestrator: orchestrator.id))
64	
65	    def validate_all(
66	        self,
67	        *,
68	        executor_registry: ExecutorRegistry | None = None,
69	    ) -> tuple[OrchestratorDefinition, ...]:
70	        for orchestrator in self._orchestrators.values():
71	            validate_orchestrator_definition(orchestrator)
72	        self._validate_child_executors(executor_registry=executor_registry)
73	        self._validate_child_orchestrators()
74	        return self.list()
75	
76	    def to_dict(self, kind: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
77	        return {"orchestrators": [orchestrator.to_dict() for orchestrator in self.list(kind=kind)]}
78	
79	    def to_json(self, *, kind: str | None = None, indent: int | None = 2) -> str:
80	        return json.dumps(self.to_dict(kind=kind), indent=indent, sort_keys=True)
81	
82	    def as_mapping(self) -> MappingProxyType[str, OrchestratorDefinition]:
83	        return MappingProxyType(dict(self._orchestrators))
84	
85	    def _validate_child_executors(self, *, executor_registry: ExecutorRegistry | None) -> None:
86	        registry = executor_registry or self._executor_registry or load_default_executor_registry()
87	        known_executor_ids = set(registry.as_mapping())
88	        for orchestrator in self._orchestrators.values():
89	            for child_executor in orchestrator.child_executors:
90	                if child_executor not in known_executor_ids:
91	                    raise OrchestratorRegistryError(
92	                        f"orchestrator {orchestrator.id!r} references unknown child executor {child_executor!r}"
93	                    )
94	
95	    def _validate_child_orchestrators(self) -> None:
96	        known_orchestrator_ids = set(self._orchestrators)
97	        graph: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {}
98	        for orchestrator in self._orchestrators.values():
99	            children: list[str] = []
100	            for child_orchestrator in orchestrator.child_orchestrators:
101	                if child_orchestrator not in known_orchestrator_ids:
102	                    raise OrchestratorRegistryError(
103	                        f"orchestrator {orchestrator.id!r} references unknown child orchestrator {child_orchestrator!r}"
104	                    )
105	                if child_orchestrator == orchestrator.id:
106	                    raise OrchestratorRegistryError(f"orchestrator {orchestrator.id!r} cannot reference itself")
107	                children.append(child_orchestrator)
108	            graph[orchestrator.id] = tuple(children)
109	        self._validate_no_cycles(graph)
110	
111	    def _validate_no_cycles(self, graph: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]) -> None:
112	        visiting: set[str] = set()
113	        visited: set[str] = set()
114	        stack: list[str] = []
115	
116	        def visit(orchestrator_id: str) -> None:
117	            if orchestrator_id in visited:
118	                return
119	            if orchestrator_id in visiting:
120	                cycle = stack[stack.index(orchestrator_id) :] + [orchestrator_id]
121	                raise OrchestratorRegistryError(f"orchestrator cycle detected: {' -> '.join(cycle)}")
122	            visiting.add(orchestrator_id)
123	            stack.append(orchestrator_id)
124	            for child in graph.get(orchestrator_id, ()):
125	                visit(child)
126	            stack.pop()
127	            visiting.remove(orchestrator_id)
128	            visited.add(orchestrator_id)
129	
130	        for orchestrator_id in sorted(graph):
131	            visit(orchestrator_id)
132	
133	
134	def load_builtin_orchestrators() -> tuple[OrchestratorDefinition, ...]:
135	    orchestrators: list[OrchestratorDefinition] = []
136	    for root in _builtin_folder_roots():
137	        orchestrators.extend(load_folder_orchestrators(root))
138	    return tuple(sorted(orchestrators, key=lambda o: o.id))
139	
140	
141	def load_curated_orchestrators() -> tuple[OrchestratorDefinition, ...]:
142	    orchestrators: list[OrchestratorDefinition] = []
143	    for path in _curated_manifest_paths():
144	        definition = load_orchestrator_manifest(path)
145	        if not _is_builtin_definition(definition):
146	            orchestrators.append(definition)
147	    for path in _curated_folder_roots():
148	        orchestrators.extend(definition for definition in load_folder_orchestrators(path) if not _is_builtin_definition(definition))
149	    return tuple(orchestrators)
150	
151	
152	def load_bundled_orchestrators() -> tuple[OrchestratorDefinition, ...]:
153	    orchestrators: list[OrchestratorDefinition] = []
154	    for path in _bundled_manifest_paths():
155	        definition = load_orchestrator_manifest(path)
156	        if not _is_builtin_definition(definition):
157	            orchestrators.append(definition)
158	    for path in _bundled_folder_roots():
159	        orchestrators.extend(definition for definition in load_folder_orchestrators(path) if not _is_builtin_definition(definition))
160	    return tuple(orchestrators)
161	
162	
163	def load_default_registry(
164	    *,
165	    executor_registry: ExecutorRegistry | None = None,
166	    banodoco_config: Any | None = None,
167	) -> OrchestratorRegistry:
168	    active_executor_registry = executor_registry
169	    registry = OrchestratorRegistry(executor_registry=active_executor_registry)
170	    for orchestrator in load_builtin_orchestrators():
171	        registry.register(orchestrator)
172	    for orchestrator in load_bundled_orchestrators():
173	        registry.register(orchestrator)
174	    for orchestrator in load_curated_orchestrators():
175	        registry.register(orchestrator)
176	    registry.validate_all(executor_registry=active_executor_registry)
177	    return registry
178	
179	
180	def _curated_manifest_paths() -> tuple[Path, ...]:
181	    return _package_manifest_paths(CURATED_PACKAGE, Path(__file__).with_name("curated"))
182	
183	
184	def _curated_folder_roots() -> tuple[Path, ...]:
185	    return _package_folder_roots(CURATED_PACKAGE, Path(__file__).with_name("curated"))
186	
187	
188	def _bundled_manifest_paths() -> tuple[Path, ...]:
189	    return _package_manifest_paths(BUNDLED_PACKAGE, Path(__file__).parents[1] / "orchestrators" / "bundled")
190	
191	
192	def _bundled_folder_roots() -> tuple[Path, ...]:
193	    return _package_folder_roots(BUNDLED_PACKAGE, Path(__file__).parents[1] / "orchestrators" / "bundled")
194	
195	
196	def _builtin_folder_roots() -> tuple[Path, ...]:
197	    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
198	    skip = {"__pycache__", "bundled", "curated"}
199	    return tuple(
200	        path
201	        for path in sorted(root.iterdir())
202	        if path.is_dir()
203	        and path.name not in skip
204	        and (path / "orchestrator.yaml").is_file()
205	        and (path / "run.py").is_file()
206	    )
207	
208	
209	def _package_manifest_paths(package: str, source_root: Path) -> tuple[Path, ...]:
210	    paths: list[Path] = []
211	    try:
212	        package_root = resources.files(package)
213	        paths.extend(Path(str(item)) for item in package_root.iterdir() if item.name.endswith(".json"))
214	    except (FileNotFoundError, ModuleNotFoundError, TypeError):
215	        pass
216	
217	    if paths:
218	        return tuple(sorted(paths))
219	
220	    if not source_root.is_dir():
221	        return ()
222	    return tuple(sorted(source_root.glob("*.json")))
223	
224	
225	def _package_folder_roots(package: str, source_root: Path) -> tuple[Path, ...]:
226	    paths: list[Path] = []
227	    try:
228	        package_root = resources.files(package)
229	        paths.extend(Path(str(item)) for item in package_root.iterdir() if item.is_dir())
230	    except (FileNotFoundError, ModuleNotFoundError, TypeError):
231	        pass
232	
233	    if paths:
234	        return tuple(sorted(paths))
235	
236	    if not source_root.is_dir():
237	        return ()
238	    return tuple(sorted(path for path in source_root.iterdir() if path.is_dir()))
239	
240	
241	def _is_builtin_definition(orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition) -> bool:
242	    return orchestrator.kind == "built_in" or orchestrator.id.startswith("builtin.")
243
```

```
1	"""Element registry and source precedence."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import os
6	import shutil
7	import sys
8	from dataclasses import dataclass
9	from importlib import resources
10	from pathlib import Path
11	from types import MappingProxyType
12	from typing import Iterable
13	
14	from artagents._paths import REPO_ROOT
15	
16	from .schema import ELEMENT_KINDS, ElementDefinition, ElementKind, ElementValidationError, load_element_definition
17	
18	
19	class ElementRegistryError(ElementValidationError):
20	    """Raised when element registry state is inconsistent."""
21	
22	
23	@dataclass(frozen=True)
24	class ElementSource:
25	    name: str
26	    root: Path
27	    priority: int
28	    editable: bool
29	
30	
31	@dataclass(frozen=True)
32	class ElementConflict:
33	    kind: ElementKind
34	    id: str
35	    winner: ElementDefinition
36	    shadowed: tuple[ElementDefinition, ...]
37	
38	
39	class ElementRegistry:
40	    """Resolved element registry keyed by kind and element id."""
41	
42	    def __init__(self, elements: Iterable[ElementDefinition] = ()) -> None:
43	        self._all: dict[tuple[str, str], list[ElementDefinition]] = {}
44	        for element in elements:
45	            self.register(element)
46	
47	    def register(self, element: ElementDefinition) -> ElementDefinition:
48	        key = (element.kind, element.id)
49	        self._all.setdefault(key, []).append(element)
50	        self._all[key].sort(key=lambda item: (item.priority, item.source, str(item.root)))
51	        return element
52	
53	    def get(self, kind: str, element_id: str) -> ElementDefinition:
54	        key = (kind, element_id)
55	        try:
56	            return self._all[key][0]
57	        except KeyError as exc:
58	            raise KeyError(f"unknown {kind} element {element_id!r}") from exc
59	
60	    def list(self, kind: str | None = None) -> tuple[ElementDefinition, ...]:
61	        if kind is not None and kind not in ELEMENT_KINDS:
62	            raise ElementRegistryError(f"kind must be one of {list(ELEMENT_KINDS)}")
63	        winners = [definitions[0] for (item_kind, _), definitions in self._all.items() if kind is None or item_kind == kind]
64	        return tuple(sorted(winners, key=lambda item: (item.kind, item.id)))
65	
66	    def conflicts(self) -> tuple[ElementConflict, ...]:
67	        conflicts: list[ElementConflict] = []
68	        for (kind, element_id), definitions in self._all.items():
69	            if len(definitions) > 1:
70	                conflicts.append(
71	                    ElementConflict(
72	                        kind=kind,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
73	                        id=element_id,
74	                        winner=definitions[0],
75	                        shadowed=tuple(definitions[1:]),
76	                    )
77	                )
78	        return tuple(sorted(conflicts, key=lambda item: (item.kind, item.id)))
79	
80	    def as_mapping(self) -> MappingProxyType[tuple[str, str], ElementDefinition]:
81	        return MappingProxyType({key: definitions[0] for key, definitions in self._all.items()})
82	
83	    def fork_target(self, kind: str, element_id: str, *, project_root: str | Path = REPO_ROOT) -> Path:
84	        element = self.get(kind, element_id)
85	        return Path(project_root) / element.fork_target
86	
87	    def fork(self, kind: str, element_id: str, *, project_root: str | Path = REPO_ROOT, overwrite: bool = False) -> Path:
88	        element = self.get(kind, element_id)
89	        target = self.fork_target(kind, element_id, project_root=project_root)
90	        if target.exists() and not overwrite:
91	            raise ElementRegistryError(f"element override already exists: {target}")
92	        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
93	        if target.exists():
94	            shutil.rmtree(target)
95	        shutil.copytree(element.root, target)
96	        return target
97	
98	
99	def load_default_registry(
100	    *,
101	    active_theme: str | Path | None = None,
102	    project_root: str | Path = REPO_ROOT,
103	    include_missing_roots: bool = False,
104	) -> ElementRegistry:
105	    registry = ElementRegistry()
106	    for source in default_sources(active_theme=active_theme, project_root=project_root):
107	        if not source.root.exists():
108	            if include_missing_roots:
109	                source.root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
110	            else:
111	                continue
112	        for element in load_source_elements(source):
113	            registry.register(element)
114	    return registry
115	
116	
117	def default_sources(*, active_theme: str | Path | None = None, project_root: str | Path = REPO_ROOT) -> tuple[ElementSource, ...]:
118	    root = Path(project_root)
119	    theme_dir = _resolve_theme_dir(active_theme)
120	    sources: list[ElementSource] = []
121	    if theme_dir is not None:
122	        sources.extend(
123	            [
124	                ElementSource("active_theme", theme_dir / "elements", 0, True),
125	                ElementSource("active_theme", theme_dir, 0, True),
126	            ]
127	        )
128	    sources.extend(
129	        [
130	            ElementSource("overrides", root / ".artagents" / "elements" / "overrides", 10, True),
131	            ElementSource("managed", root / ".artagents" / "elements" / "managed", 20, False),
132	            ElementSource("bundled", _bundled_root(), 30, False),
133	        ]
134	    )
135	    return tuple(sources)
136	
137	
138	def load_source_elements(source: ElementSource) -> tuple[ElementDefinition, ...]:
139	    elements: list[ElementDefinition] = []
140	    for kind in ELEMENT_KINDS:
141	        kind_root = source.root / kind
142	        if not kind_root.is_dir():
143	            continue
144	        for child in sorted(kind_root.iterdir(), key=lambda path: path.name):
145	            if child.is_dir():
146	                try:
147	                    elements.append(
148	                        load_element_definition(
149	                            child,
150	                            kind=kind,
151	                            source=source.name,
152	                            editable=source.editable,
153	                            priority=source.priority,
154	                        )
155	                    )
156	                except ElementValidationError as exc:
157	                    print(f"WARN skipping {child}: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
158	    return tuple(elements)
159	
160	
161	def _resolve_theme_dir(theme: str | Path | None) -> Path | None:
162	    raw = os.environ.get("HYPE_ACTIVE_THEME") if theme is None else theme
163	    if not raw:
164	        return None
165	    candidate = Path(raw).expanduser()
166	    if candidate.name == "theme.json":
167	        return candidate.parent.resolve()
168	    if candidate.exists():
169	        return (candidate if candidate.is_dir() else candidate.parent).resolve()
170	    return (REPO_ROOT.parent / "themes" / str(raw)).resolve()
171	
172	
173	def _bundled_root() -> Path:
174	    try:
175	        return Path(str(resources.files("artagents.elements.bundled")))
176	    except (ModuleNotFoundError, TypeError):
177	        return Path(__file__).with_name("bundled")
```

We are scoping a migration that moves all registered "things" (executors, orchestrators, elements) from `artagents/executors/<name>/`, `artagents/orchestrators/<name>/`, `artagents/elements/bundled/<name>/` into `artagents/packs/builtin/<name>/`. Each thing is a folder identified by a manifest file like `executor.yaml`, `orchestrator.yaml`, or similar.

Your job: inventory the CONTENT (not the framework code) that would need to move.

Produce three lists:
1. Every executor folder under `artagents/executors/` — folder name + what files it contains (executor.yaml, run.py, STAGE.md, requirements.txt, pyproject.toml, etc).
2. Every orchestrator folder under `artagents/orchestrators/` — same format.
3. Every element folder under `artagents/elements/` — same format.

For each, flag:
- Folders with non-standard structure (extra subdirs, missing manifest, unusual files)
- Folders that have their own `pyproject.toml` or `requirements.txt` (these have isolated deps)
- Folders with submodules or local helpers that other folders import from
- Any folder that looks like a "default/test" rather than real content
- Any folder where the manifest filename differs from the kind convention

Also note: are there any registered things stored OUTSIDE these three directories that we'd be missing?

Report as compact lists with counts at the top (e.g. "32 executors, 6 orchestrators, 4 elements"). Under 500 words. The point is a clean inventory so we can size the mechanical-move work.

```
30	def discover_folder_executor_roots(root: str | Path) -> tuple[Path, ...]:
31	    """Return folders under `root` that contain an executor manifest or executor.py."""
32	
33	    search_root = Path(root)
34	    if not search_root.is_dir():
35	        return ()
36	    roots = {
37	        path.parent.resolve()
38	        for path in search_root.rglob("*")
39	        if _is_executor_folder_file(path)
40	    }
41	    return tuple(sorted(roots))

46

48	    for executor_root in discover_folder_executor_roots(root):
49	        executors.extend(_load_folder_executor_definitions(executor_root))
```

```
14	    OrchestratorValidationError,
15	    load_orchestrator_manifest,
16	    validate_orchestrator_definition,
17	)
18	
19	
20	_RESULT_PREFIX = "__ARTAGENTS_ORCHESTRATOR_METADATA__="
21	_MANIFEST_NAMES = ("orchestrator.yaml", "orchestrator.yml", "orchestrator.json")
22	
23	
24	class FolderOrchestratorError(OrchestratorValidationError):
25	    """Raised when a folder orchestrator cannot be discovered or extracted."""
26	
27	
28	def discover_folder_orchestrator_roots(root: str | Path) -> tuple[Path, ...]:
29	    """Return folders under `root` that contain orchestrator metadata."""
30	
31	    search_root = Path(root)
32	    if not search_root.is_dir():
33	        return ()
34	    roots = {path.parent.resolve() for path in search_root.rglob("orchestrator.py") if _is_orchestrator_file(path)}
35	    for manifest_name in _MANIFEST_NAMES:
36	        roots.update(path.parent.resolve() for path in search_root.rglob(manifest_name) if _is_orchestrator_file(path))
37	    return tuple(sorted(roots))
38	
39	
40	def load_folder_orchestrators(root: str | Path) -> tuple[OrchestratorDefinition, ...]:
41	    """Load every folder orchestrator below `root` through the subprocess extractor."""
42	
43	    orchestrators: list[OrchestratorDefinition] = []
44	    for orchestrator_root in discover_folder_orchestrator_roots(root):
45	        orchestrators.extend(_load_folder_orchestrator_definitions(orchestrator_root))
46	    return tuple(orchestrators)
47	
48	
49	def load_folder_orchestrator(orchestrator_root: str | Path) -> OrchestratorDefinition:
50	    """Extract and validate a single folder orchestrator without importing it here."""
```

```
36	def _builtin_steps_by_name() -> Mapping[str, Any]:
37	    pipeline = _pipeline_module()
38	    steps = {step.name: step for step in pipeline.build_pool_steps()}
39	    missing = [name for name in pipeline.STEP_ORDER if name not in steps]
40	    if missing:
```

Your job: map the CLI and id surface so we can scope what the migration breaks for users.

```
1	"""Local setup planner for ArtAgents."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import argparse
6	import json
7	from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
8	from pathlib import Path
9	
10	from artagents._paths import REPO_ROOT
11	from artagents.elements.cli import _sync_managed_defaults
12	from artagents.elements.install import install_element
13	from artagents.elements.registry import load_default_registry as load_element_registry
14	
15	
16	@dataclass(frozen=True)
17	class SetupStep:
18	    name: str
19	    status: str
20	    detail: str
21	
22	
23	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
24	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python3 -m artagents setup", description="Plan or apply ArtAgents local setup.")
25	    parser.add_argument("--apply", action="store_true", help="Apply local setup mutations. Default is dry-run.")
26	    parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Emit machine-readable setup output.")
27	    return parser
28	
29	
30	def plan_setup(*, apply: bool = False, project_root: str | Path | None = None) -> tuple[SetupStep, ...]:
31	    root = Path(project_root or REPO_ROOT)
32	    steps: list[SetupStep] = []
33	    steps.append(
34	        SetupStep(
35	            name="mode",
36	            status="apply" if apply else "dry-run",
37	            detail="local mutations enabled" if apply else "no files or dependencies will be changed",
38	        )
39	    )
40	    steps.append(_plan_agents_symlink(root, apply=apply))
41	    for action in _sync_managed_defaults(dry_run=not apply, overwrite=False, project_root=root):
42	        steps.append(SetupStep(name="elements sync", status="applied" if apply else "planned", detail=action))
43	
44	    registry = load_element_registry(project_root=root)
45	    for element in registry.list():
46	        result = install_element(element, project_root=root, dry_run=not apply)
47	        plan = result.plan
48	        if plan.noop_reason:
49	            steps.append(SetupStep(name="elements install", status="skipped", detail=f"{element.kind}/{element.id}: {plan.noop_reason}"))
50	            continue
51	        status = "applied" if apply else "planned"
52	        details = "; ".join(plan.command_lines())
53	        steps.append(SetupStep(name="elements install", status=status, detail=f"{element.kind}/{element.id}: {details}"))
54	    return tuple(steps)
55	
56	
57	def _plan_agents_symlink(root: Path, *, apply: bool) -> SetupStep:
58	    """Ensure AGENTS.md is a symlink to SKILL.md so both loaders read the same source."""
59	    agents = root / "AGENTS.md"
60	    target = "SKILL.md"
61	    skill = root / target
62	    if not skill.is_file():
63	        return SetupStep(name="agents.md symlink", status="warn", detail=f"{skill} missing; cannot link AGENTS.md")
64	    if agents.is_symlink() and (root / agents.readlink()).resolve() == skill.resolve():
65	        return SetupStep(name="agents.md symlink", status="ok", detail=f"AGENTS.md → {target}")
66	    if not agents.exists() and not agents.is_symlink():
67	        if apply:
68	            agents.symlink_to(target)
69	            return SetupStep(name="agents.md symlink", status="applied", detail=f"created AGENTS.md → {target}")
70	        return SetupStep(name="agents.md symlink", status="planned", detail=f"will create AGENTS.md → {target}")
71	    kind = "wrong symlink" if agents.is_symlink() else "regular file"
72	    if apply:
73	        agents.unlink()
74	        agents.symlink_to(target)
75	        return SetupStep(name="agents.md symlink", status="applied", detail=f"replaced AGENTS.md ({kind}) with symlink → {target}")
76	    return SetupStep(name="agents.md symlink", status="planned", detail=f"will replace AGENTS.md ({kind}) with symlink → {target}")
77	
78	
79	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
80	    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
81	    steps = plan_setup(apply=bool(args.apply))
82	    if args.json:
83	        payload = {
84	            "applied": bool(args.apply),
85	            "steps": [asdict(step) for step in steps],
86	        }
87	        print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
88	        return 0
89	
90	    print("ArtAgents setup")
91	    if not args.apply:
92	        print("dry-run: pass --apply to sync managed defaults and run local element install commands")
93	    for step in steps:
94	        print(f"[{step.status}] {step.name}: {step.detail}")
95	    return 0
96	
97	
98	if __name__ == "__main__":
99	    raise SystemExit(main())
100
```

```
17	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
18	    parser = build_parser()
19	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
20	    try:
21	        registry = load_default_registry(_banodoco_config_from_args(args))
22	        return int(args.handler(args, registry))
23	    except (KeyError, ExecutorValidationError, ValueError) as exc:
24	        print(f"executors: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
25	        return 2
26	
27	
28	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
29	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
30	        prog="python3 -m artagents executors",
31	        description="List, inspect, validate, install, and run ArtAgents executors.",
32	        formatter_class=argparse.RawTextHelpFormatter,
33	    )
34	    parser.add_argument("--banodoco-agent-executors", action="store_true", help="Opt in to loading executors from the Banodoco website catalog.")
35	    parser.add_argument("--banodoco-catalog-url", help="Banodoco website agent-executor catalog Edge Function URL.")
36	    parser.add_argument("--banodoco-cache-dir", help="Cache directory for git-backed Banodoco executors.")
37	    parser.add_argument("--banodoco-refresh", action="store_true", help="Refresh cached git checkouts before loading Banodoco executors.")
38	    parser.add_argument("--no-banodoco-defaults", action="store_true", help="Skip Banodoco catalog executors marked default.")
39	    parser.add_argument("--no-banodoco-mandatory", action="store_true", help="Skip Banodoco catalog executors marked mandatory.")
40	    subparsers = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
41	
42	    list_parser = subparsers.add_parser("list", help="List available executors.")
43	    list_parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Emit machine-readable JSON.")
44	    list_parser.add_argument("--kind", choices=("built_in", "external"), help="Filter executors by kind.")
45	    list_parser.set_defaults(handler=_cmd_list)
46	
47	    inspect_parser = subparsers.add_parser("inspect", help="Inspect one executor.")
48	    inspect_parser.add_argument("executor_id")
49	    inspect_parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Emit machine-readable JSON.")
50	    inspect_parser.set_defaults(handler=_cmd_inspect)
51	
52	    validate_parser = subparsers.add_parser("validate", help="Validate executor metadata.")
53	    validate_parser.add_argument("executor_id", nargs="?")
54	    validate_parser.add_argument("--check-binaries", action="store_true", help="Also require declared external binaries to be on PATH.")
55	    validate_parser.set_defaults(handler=_cmd_validate)
56	
57	    install_parser = subparsers.add_parser("install", help="Install dependencies for one executor.")
58	    install_parser.add_argument("executor_id")
59	    install_parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Print install commands without executing them.")
60	    install_parser.set_defaults(handler=_cmd_install)
61	
62	    run_parser = subparsers.add_parser("run", help="Run or dry-run one executor.")
63	    run_parser.add_argument("executor_id")
64	    run_parser.add_argument("--out", help="Output directory for runtime placeholders.")
65	    run_parser.add_argument("--input", action="append", default=[], metavar="NAME=VALUE", help="Executor input value; may be repeated.")
66	    run_parser.add_argument("--brief", help="Brief path for built-in pipeline context synthesis.")
67	    run_parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Build and print the command without executing it.")
68	    run_parser.add_argument("--check-binaries", action="store_true", help="Also require declared external binaries to be on PATH.")
69	    run_parser.add_argument("--python-exec", help="Python executable for {python_exec} placeholders.")
70	    run_parser.add_argument("--verbose", action="store_true", help="Stream subprocess output for built-in pipeline steps.")
71	    run_parser.add_argument("--thread", help="Thread id, @new, or @none for this run.")
72	    run_parser.add_argument("--variants", type=int, help="Request a sibling variant count for variant-aware producers.")
73	    run_parser.add_argument("--from", dest="from_ref", help="Consume a specific prior run or variant, e.g. <run-id>:<n>.")

86	def _banodoco_config_from_args(args: argparse.Namespace) -> BanodocoCatalogConfig:

100	def _cmd_list(args: argparse.Namespace, registry: ExecutorRegistry) -> int:
101	    executors = registry.list(kind=args.kind)
102	    if args.json:

104	        return 0

110	def _cmd_inspect(args: argparse.Namespace, registry: ExecutorRegistry) -> int:
111	    executor = registry.get(args.executor_id)
112	    if args.json:
113	        print(executor.to_json())
114	        return 0
115	    print(f"id: {executor.id}")
116	    print(f"name: {executor.name}")
117	    print(f"kind: {executor.kind}")

119	    if executor.description:
120	        print(f"description: {executor.description}")
121	    _print_ports("inputs", executor.inputs)
122	    _print_outputs(executor)
123	    if executor.command is not None:

125	    print(f"cache: {executor.cache.mode}")
126	    if executor.cache.sentinels:
127	        print(f"cache_sentinels: {', '.join(executor.cache.sentinels)}")
128	    if executor.isolation.binaries:
129	        print(f"binaries: {', '.join(executor.isolation.binaries)}")
130	    return 0
131	
132	
133	def _cmd_validate(args: argparse.Namespace, registry: ExecutorRegistry) -> int:
134	    registry.validate_all()
135	    executors = [registry.get(args.executor_id)] if args.executor_id else registry.list()
136	    missing_by_executor: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {}
137	    if args.check_binaries:
138	        from .runner import check_executor_binaries
139	
140	        for executor in executors:
141	            missing = check_executor_binaries(executor)
142	            if missing:
143	                missing_by_executor[executor.id] = missing
144	    if missing_by_executor:
145	        for executor_id, missing in missing_by_executor.items():
146	            print(f"{executor_id}: missing binaries: {', '.join(missing)}", file=sys.stderr)
147	        return 1
148	    if args.executor_id:
149	        print(f"{args.executor_id}: ok")
150	    else:
151	        print(f"{len(executors)} executor(s): ok")
152	    return 0
153	
154	
155	def _cmd_install(args: argparse.Namespace, registry: ExecutorRegistry) -> int:
156	    from .install import install_executor
157	
158	    executor = registry.get(args.executor_id)
159	    result = install_executor(executor, dry_run=bool(args.dry_run))
160	    plan = result.plan
161	    if plan.noop_reason:
162	        print(f"{executor.id}: no install needed: {plan.noop_reason}")
163	        return result.returncode
164	    if plan.environment_path is not None:
165	        print(f"env: {plan.environment_path}")
166	    if plan.python_path is not None:
167	        print(f"python: {plan.python_path}")
168	    for command in plan.commands:
169	        print(shlex.join(command))
170	    return result.returncode
171	
172	
173	def _cmd_run(args: argparse.Namespace, registry: ExecutorRegistry) -> int:
174	    from .runner import ExecutorRunRequest, run_executor
175	
176	    executor = registry.get(args.executor_id)
177	    if not args.out and _executor_needs_out(executor):
178	        raise ValueError("--out is required for this executor")
179	    request = ExecutorRunRequest(
180	        executor_id=args.executor_id,
181	        out=Path(args.out) if args.out else "",
182	        inputs=_run_inputs(args),
183	        brief=Path(args.brief) if args.brief else None,
184	        dry_run=bool(args.dry_run),
185	        check_binaries=bool(args.check_binaries),
186	        python_exec=args.python_exec,
187	        verbose=bool(args.verbose),
188	        thread=args.thread,
189	        variants=args.variants,
190	        from_ref=args.from_ref,
191	    )
192	    result = run_executor(request, registry)
193	    if result.missing_binaries:
194	        print(f"{args.executor_id}: missing binaries: {', '.join(result.missing_binaries)}", file=sys.stderr)
195	        return 1
196	    if result.skipped:
197	        print(f"{args.executor_id}: skipped: {result.skipped_reason}")
198	        return 0
199	    if result.command:
200	        print(shlex.join(result.command))
201	    if result.payload:
202	        print(json.dumps(dict(result.payload), separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True))
203	    return int(result.returncode or 0)
204	
205	
206	def _executor_needs_out(executor: ExecutorDefinition) -> bool:
207	    if executor.id == "upload.youtube":
208	        return False
209	    if executor.kind == "built_in" and "pipeline_step" in executor.metadata:
210	        return True
211	    if executor.command is not None:
212	        parts = [*executor.command.argv]
213	        if executor.command.cwd:
214	            parts.append(executor.command.cwd)
215	        parts.extend(executor.command.env.values())
216	        if any("{out}" in part for part in parts):
217	            return True
218	    return any((output.path_template and "{out}" in output.path_template) for output in executor.outputs)
219	
220	
221	def _run_inputs(args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict[str, Any]:
222	    inputs = _parse_input_values(args.input)
223	    for key in ("video_url", "title", "description", "privacy_status", "playlist_id"):
224	        value = getattr(args, key)
225	        if value not in (None, ""):
226	            inputs[key] = value
227	    tags = [*getattr(args, "tag", []), *getattr(args, "tags", [])]
228	    if tags:
229	        inputs["tags"] = tags
230	    if getattr(args, "made_for_kids", False):
231	        inputs["made_for_kids"] = True
232	    return inputs
233	
234	
235	def _parse_input_values(raw_values: list[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
236	    values: dict[str, str] = {}
237	    for raw in raw_values:
238	        if "=" not in raw:
239	            raise ValueError(f"invalid --input value {raw!r}; expected NAME=VALUE")
240	        key, value = raw.split("=", 1)
241	        key = key.strip()
242	        if not key:
243	            raise ValueError(f"invalid --input value {raw!r}; expected NAME=VALUE")
244	        values[key] = value
245	    return values
246	
247	
248	def _print_ports(label: str, ports: tuple[Any, ...]) -> None:
249	    if not ports:
250	        return
251	    print(f"{label}:")
252	    for port in ports:
253	        required = "required" if port.required else "optional"
254	        print(f"  - {port.name} ({port.type}, {required})")
255	
256	
257	def _print_outputs(executor: ExecutorDefinition) -> None:
258	    if not executor.outputs:
259	        return
260	    print("outputs:")
261	    for output in executor.outputs:
262	        placeholder = f", placeholder={output.placeholder}" if output.placeholder else ""
263	        print(f"  - {output.name} ({output.type}, {output.mode}{placeholder})")
264	
265	
266	if __name__ == "__main__":
267	    raise SystemExit(main())
268
```

```
18	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
19	    parser = build_parser()
20	    parse_argv, passthrough = _split_run_passthrough(list(argv) if argv is not None else sys.argv[1:])
21	    args = parser.parse_args(parse_argv)
22	    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "run":
23	        args.orchestrator_args = passthrough

25	        registry = load_default_registry(banodoco_config=_banodoco_config_from_args(args))

32	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:

166	def _split_run_passthrough(argv: list[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
167	    if not argv or argv[0] != "run" or "--" not in argv:
168	        return argv, []
169	    separator_index = argv.index("--")
170	    return argv[:separator_index], argv[separator_index + 1 :]

172	
173	def _print_run_result(result: Any) -> None:
174	    commands = result.planned_commands or ((result.command,) if result.command else ())
175	    for command in commands:
176	        if command:
177	            print(shlex.join(command))
178	    if result.errors:
179	        for error in result.errors:
180	            print(f"{error.kind}: {error.message}", file=sys.stderr)
181	
182	
183	def _print_ports(label: str, ports: tuple[Any, ...]) -> None:
184	    if not ports:
185	        return
186	    print(f"{label}:")
187	    for port in ports:
188	        required = "required" if port.required else "optional"
189	        print(f"  - {port.name} ({port.type}, {required})")
190	
191	
192	def _print_outputs(orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition) -> None:
193	    if not orchestrator.outputs:
194	        return
195	    print("outputs:")
196	    for output in orchestrator.outputs:
197	        placeholder = f", placeholder={output.placeholder}" if output.placeholder else ""
198	        print(f"  - {output.name} ({output.type}, {output.mode}{placeholder})")
199	
200	
201	if __name__ == "__main__":
202	    raise SystemExit(main())
203
```

```
19	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
20	    parser = build_parser()
21	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
22	    try:
23	        registry = load_default_registry(active_theme=args.theme, project_root=REPO_ROOT)
24	        return int(args.handler(args, registry))
25	    except (KeyError, ElementRegistryError, ElementValidationError, ValueError) as exc:
26	        print(f"elements: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
27	        return 2

30	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
31	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
32	        prog="python3 -m artagents elements",
33	        description="List, inspect, validate, sync, fork, install, and update ArtAgents render elements.",
34	    )
35	    parser.add_argument("--theme", help="Active theme id, theme directory, or path to theme.json.")
36	    subparsers = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
37	
38	    list_parser = subparsers.add_parser("list", help="List available elements.")
39	    list_parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Emit machine-readable JSON.")
40	    list_parser.add_argument("--kind", choices=ELEMENT_KINDS, help="Filter by element kind.")
41	    list_parser.set_defaults(handler=_cmd_list)
42	
43	    inspect_parser = subparsers.add_parser("inspect", help="Inspect one element.")
44	    inspect_parser.add_argument("kind", choices=ELEMENT_KINDS)
45	    inspect_parser.add_argument("element_id")
46	    inspect_parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Emit machine-readable JSON.")
47	    inspect_parser.set_defaults(handler=_cmd_inspect)
48	
49	    validate_parser = subparsers.add_parser("validate", help="Validate element metadata.")
50	    validate_parser.add_argument("kind", choices=ELEMENT_KINDS, nargs="?")
51	    validate_parser.add_argument("element_id", nargs="?")
52	    validate_parser.set_defaults(handler=_cmd_validate)
53	
54	    sync_parser = subparsers.add_parser("sync", help="Materialize bundled defaults into .artagents/elements/managed.")
55	    sync_parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Print actions without copying files.")
56	    sync_parser.set_defaults(handler=_cmd_sync)
57	
58	    fork_parser = subparsers.add_parser("fork", help="Fork an element into .artagents/elements/overrides.")
59	    fork_parser.add_argument("kind", choices=ELEMENT_KINDS)
60	    fork_parser.add_argument("element_id")
61	    fork_parser.add_argument("--overwrite", action="store_true", help="Replace an existing override.")
62	    fork_parser.set_defaults(handler=_cmd_fork)
63	
64	    install_parser = subparsers.add_parser("install", help="Plan or apply local dependency install for one element.")
65	    install_parser.add_argument("kind", choices=ELEMENT_KINDS)
66	    install_parser.add_argument("element_id")
67	    install_parser.add_argument("--apply", action="store_true", help="Run the local install commands. Default is dry-run.")
68	    install_parser.set_defaults(handler=_cmd_install)
69	
70	    update_parser = subparsers.add_parser("update", help="Refresh managed defaults without overwriting overrides.")
71	    update_parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Print actions without copying files.")
72	    update_parser.set_defaults(handler=_cmd_update)
73	    return parser

76	def _cmd_list(args: argparse.Namespace, registry: Any) -> int:
77	    elements = registry.list(kind=args.kind)
78	    if args.json:
79	        print(json.dumps({"elements": [element.to_dict() for element in elements]}, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
80	        return 0
81	    for element in elements:
82	        print(f"{element.kind}\t{element.id}\t{element.source}\t{'editable' if element.editable else 'managed'}")
83	    return 0
84	
85	
86	def _cmd_inspect(args: argparse.Namespace, registry: Any) -> int:
87	    element = registry.get(args.kind, args.element_id)
88	    if args.json:
89	        print(element.to_json())
90	        return 0
91	    print(f"id: {element.id}")
92	    print(f"kind: {element.kind}")
93	    print(f"source: {element.source}")
94	    print(f"editable: {str(element.editable).lower()}")
95	    print(f"root: {element.root}")
96	    print(f"fork_target: {element.fork_target}")
97	    return 0
98	
99	
100	def _cmd_validate(args: argparse.Namespace, registry: Any) -> int:
101	    if args.kind and args.element_id:
102	        registry.get(args.kind, args.element_id)
103	        print(f"{args.kind}/{args.element_id}: ok")
104	        return 0
105	    elements = registry.list(kind=args.kind)
106	    print(f"{len(elements)} element(s): ok")
107	    return 0
108	
109	
110	def _cmd_sync(args: argparse.Namespace, registry: Any) -> int:
111	    del registry
112	    actions = _sync_managed_defaults(dry_run=bool(args.dry_run), overwrite=False)
113	    _print_actions(actions)
114	    return 0
115	
116	
117	def _cmd_update(args: argparse.Namespace, registry: Any) -> int:
118	    del registry
119	    actions = _sync_managed_defaults(dry_run=bool(args.dry_run), overwrite=True)
120	    _print_actions(actions)
121	    return 0
122	
123	
124	def _cmd_fork(args: argparse.Namespace, registry: Any) -> int:
125	    target = registry.fork(args.kind, args.element_id, project_root=REPO_ROOT, overwrite=bool(args.overwrite))
126	    print(f"forked: {target}")
127	    return 0
128	
129	
130	def _cmd_install(args: argparse.Namespace, registry: Any) -> int:
131	    element = registry.get(args.kind, args.element_id)
132	    result = install_element(element, project_root=REPO_ROOT, dry_run=not bool(args.apply))
133	    plan = result.plan
134	    if plan.noop_reason:
135	        print(f"{element.kind}/{element.id}: no install needed: {plan.noop_reason}")
136	        return result.returncode
137	    print(f"root: {plan.root}")
138	    if plan.venv_path is not None:
139	        print(f"venv: {plan.venv_path}")
140	    if plan.node_prefix is not None:
141	        print(f"node: {plan.node_prefix}")
142	    for line in plan.command_lines():
143	        print(line)
144	    if not args.apply:
145	        print("dry-run: pass --apply to run these local install commands")
146	    return result.returncode
147	
148	
149	def _sync_managed_defaults(*, dry_run: bool, overwrite: bool, project_root: Path | None = None) -> list[str]:
150	    project_root = project_root or REPO_ROOT
151	    bundled_root = None
152	    for source in default_sources(project_root=project_root):
153	        if source.name == "bundled":
154	            bundled_root = source.root
155	            break
156	    if bundled_root is None:
157	        raise ElementRegistryError("bundled element root is not configured")
158	    managed_root = project_root / ".artagents" / "elements" / "managed"
159	    override_root = project_root / ".artagents" / "elements" / "overrides"
160	    actions: list[str] = []
161	    for kind in ELEMENT_KINDS:
162	        source_kind = bundled_root / kind
163	        if not source_kind.is_dir():
164	            continue
165	        for source_element in sorted(source_kind.iterdir(), key=lambda path: path.name):
166	            if not source_element.is_dir():
167	                continue
168	            target = managed_root / kind / source_element.name
169	            override = override_root / kind / source_element.name
170	            if override.exists():
171	                actions.append(f"skip override: {override}")
172	                continue
173	            if target.exists() and not overwrite:
174	                actions.append(f"exists: {target}")
175	                continue
176	            if dry_run:
177	                actions.append(("update: " if target.exists() else "sync: ") + str(target))
178	                continue
179	            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
180	            if target.exists():
181	                shutil.rmtree(target)
182	            shutil.copytree(source_element, target)
183	            actions.append(("updated: " if overwrite else "synced: ") + str(target))
184	    if not actions:
185	        actions.append("no managed defaults to sync")
186	    return actions
187	
188	
189	def _print_actions(actions: list[str]) -> None:
190	    for action in actions:
191	        print(action)
192	
193	
194	if __name__ == "__main__":
195	    raise SystemExit(main())
196
```

```
125	def _canonical_child_list(data: dict[str, Any], *, legacy_key: str, canonical_key: str, path: str) -> list[str]:
126	    legacy_present = legacy_key in data
127	    canonical_present = canonical_key in data
128	    legacy_values = _optional_string_list(data, legacy_key, f"orchestrator.{legacy_key}") if legacy_present else []
129	    canonical_values = _optional_string_list(data, canonical_key, f"orchestrator.{canonical_key}") if canonical_present else []
130	    if legacy_present and canonical_present and legacy_values != canonical_values:
131	        raise OrchestratorValidationError(
132	            f"orchestrator.{legacy_key} and orchestrator.{canonical_key} conflict; use identical values or only one field"
133	        )
134	    return canonical_values if canonical_present else legacy_values
```

We are scoping a migration that moves all registered things from `artagents/executors/<name>/`, `artagents/orchestrators/<name>/`, `artagents/elements/<name>/` into `artagents/packs/<pack>/<name>/`, and may collapse three parallel framework dirs into one `artagents/core/`. Ids would become qualified (e.g. `builtin.cut` instead of `cut`).

Your job: map the TEST surface so we can estimate test breakage and update cost.

Investigate the `tests/` directory:

Report a numbered breakdown (X tests would break, Y are safe, Z are unclear) with rationale. Under 500 words. List the breakage-prone tests by filename.

```
42	@dataclass(frozen=True)
43	class StructureReport:
44	    errors: tuple[str, ...]
45	
46	    @property
47	    def ok(self) -> bool:
48	        return not self.errors
49	
50	
51	def validate_repo_structure(root: str | Path = REPO_ROOT) -> StructureReport:
52	    repo_root = Path(root)
53	    errors: list[str] = []
54	    errors.extend(_validate_legacy_dirs(repo_root))
55	    errors.extend(_validate_local_state_dirs(repo_root))
56	    errors.extend(_validate_top_level_artagents(repo_root / "artagents"))
57	    errors.extend(_validate_executor_folders(repo_root / "artagents" / "executors"))
58	    errors.extend(_validate_orchestrator_folders(repo_root / "artagents" / "orchestrators"))
59	    return StructureReport(errors=tuple(errors))
60	
61	
62	def _validate_legacy_dirs(repo_root: Path) -> list[str]:
63	    errors: list[str] = []
64	    for dirname in LEGACY_PUBLIC_DIRS:
65	        candidate = repo_root / "artagents" / dirname
66	        if candidate.exists():
67	            errors.append(f"legacy public package must not exist: {candidate.relative_to(repo_root)}")
68	    return errors
69	
70	
71	def _validate_local_state_dirs(repo_root: Path) -> list[str]:
72	    errors: list[str] = []
73	    local_root = repo_root / ".artagents"
74	    if not local_root.exists():
75	        return errors
76	    for dirname in LEGACY_LOCAL_DIRS:
77	        candidate = local_root / dirname
78	        if candidate.exists():
79	            errors.append(f"legacy local state directory must not exist: {candidate.relative_to(repo_root)}")
80	    return errors
81	
82	
83	def _validate_top_level_artagents(package_root: Path) -> list[str]:
84	    errors: list[str] = []
85	    for child in sorted(package_root.iterdir()):
86	        if child.name.startswith("."):
87	            continue
88	        if child.is_file() and child.suffix == ".py" and child.name not in TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_FILES:
89	            errors.append(f"top-level artagents module must move to a canonical package: {child.relative_to(package_root.parents[0])}")
90	        if child.is_dir() and child.name not in TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS:
91	            errors.append(f"top-level artagents directory is not a canonical concept: {child.relative_to(package_root.parents[0])}")
92	    return errors
93

99	    errors: list[str] = []
```

Repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents

We are scoping a migration that introduces "packs" as a grouping layer. After the migration, executor ids become `<pack>.<name>` and venvs would live at `.artagents/executors/<pack>.<name>/venv` (or similar). We are also considering pack-level `pyproject.toml` for shared deps across executors in a pack.

Your job: map the INSTALL/VENV lifecycle so we can scope the changes.

Read carefully:
- `artagents/executors/install.py` — full file
- `artagents/orchestrators/install.py` if exists
- `artagents/elements/install.py` if exists
- The relevant CLI install commands

Then answer:
1. Where do venvs live today? What is the safe-id derivation logic?
2. What's the dep resolution order? (executor.yaml `isolation.requirements` → pyproject.toml → requirements.txt → ?)
3. How does the install command invoke uv? Per-executor only, or bulk?
4. Where does `.artagents/` live on disk and what does its structure look like? Is it gitignored?
5. Are there cached install-state files (lockfiles, manifest hashes) that pin to current paths?
6. What's the runtime behavior — when an executor runs, how is its venv resolved? Path lookup? Subprocess invocation? See `artagents/executors/runner.py`.
7. Is there any code that would need to handle "pack-level shared venv" (one venv used by multiple executors in a pack)? How invasive would adding that be?
8. How would existing users' installed venvs at `.artagents/executors/<bare-name>/venv` interact with new path `<pack>.<bare-name>/venv` — silent reinstall, error, or migration script needed?

Report under 500 words with file_path:line_number references. Focus on what changes versus stays the same.

```
53	def executor_environment_path(executor: ExecutorDefinition) -> Path:
54	    """Return the deterministic repo-local virtualenv path for a executor."""
55	
56	    return REPO_ROOT / ".artagents" / "executors" / _safe_executor_id(_executor_install_id(executor)) / "venv"

59	def executor_python_path(executor: ExecutorDefinition) -> Path:
60	    """Return the Python executable path inside a executor virtualenv."""
61	
62	    env_path = executor_environment_path(executor)
63	    if sys.platform == "win32":
64	        return env_path / "Scripts" / "python.exe"
65	    return env_path / "bin" / "python"
66	
67	
68	def build_executor_install_plan(executor: ExecutorDefinition) -> ExecutorInstallPlan:
69	    """Build uv commands for explicit executor installation."""
70	
71	    if executor.kind == "built_in":
72	        return ExecutorInstallPlan(
73	            executor_id=executor.id,
74	            kind=executor.kind,
75	            environment_path=None,
76	            python_path=None,
77	            noop_reason="built-in executors use the host Python environment",
78	        )
79	    if executor.kind != "external":
80	        raise ExecutorInstallError(f"unsupported executor kind {executor.kind!r}")
81	
82	    env_path = executor_environment_path(executor)
83	    python_path = executor_python_path(executor)
84	    commands: list[tuple[str, ...]] = [("uv", "venv", str(env_path))]
85	    dependency_command = _dependency_install_command(executor, python_path)
86	    if dependency_command is not None:
87	        commands.append(dependency_command)
88	    return ExecutorInstallPlan(
89	        executor_id=executor.id,
90	        kind=executor.kind,
91	        environment_path=env_path,
92	        python_path=python_path,
93	        commands=tuple(commands),

97	def install_executor(executor: ExecutorDefinition, *, dry_run: bool = False) -> ExecutorInstallResult:
98	    """Install dependencies for a executor, or return the dry-run plan."""
99	
100	    plan = build_executor_install_plan(executor)
101	    if dry_run or not plan.commands:
102	        return ExecutorInstallResult(plan=plan, dry_run=dry_run)
103	    for command in plan.commands:
104	        completed = subprocess.run(list(command), check=False)
105	        if completed.returncode != 0:
106	            return ExecutorInstallResult(plan=plan, dry_run=False, returncode=completed.returncode)
107	    return ExecutorInstallResult(plan=plan, dry_run=False)

136	class _GitManifestSource:
137	    repo_url: str
138	    manifest_path: str
139	    expected_id: str
140	    commit_sha: str | None = None
141	    tag: str | None = None
142	    branch: str | None = None
143	    source_ref: str | None = None
144	    install_subdir: str | None = None
145	
146	
147	def _fetch_git_manifest(
148	    source: _GitManifestSource,
149	    *,
150	    cache_root: Path,
151	    refresh: bool,
152	    label: str,
153	) -> dict:
154	    _validate_git_manifest_source(source, label=label)
155	    checkout = cache_root / _safe_manifest_source_dir(source)
156	    if refresh and checkout.exists():
157	        shutil.rmtree(checkout.parent)
158	    checkout.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
159	
160	    if not checkout.exists():
161	        _clone_git_manifest_source(source, checkout)
162	    elif refresh:
163	        _run_git(("git", "-C", str(checkout), "fetch", "--tags", "--prune"))
164	        _checkout_git_manifest_ref(source, checkout)
165	
166	    manifest_path = _safe_child_path(checkout, source.manifest_path)
167	    if not manifest_path.is_file():
168	        raise ExecutorInstallError(f"git {label} manifest not found: {manifest_path}")
169	    try:
170	        manifest = loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
171	    except ValueError as exc:
172	        raise ExecutorInstallError(f"invalid git {label} manifest JSON: {manifest_path}") from exc
173	    if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
174	        raise ExecutorInstallError(f"git {label} manifest must be a JSON object")
175	    manifest_id = manifest.get("id")
176	    if manifest_id != source.expected_id:
177	        raise ExecutorInstallError(
178	            f"git {label} identity mismatch: expected {source.expected_id!r}, fetched {manifest_id!r}"
179	        )
180	    return manifest

183	def _dependency_install_command(executor: ExecutorDefinition, python_path: Path) -> tuple[str, ...] | None:
184	    pyproject_file = _metadata_path(executor, "pyproject_file")
185	    if pyproject_file is not None:
186	        install_target = _metadata_path(executor, "executor_root") or pyproject_file.parent
187	        return ("uv", "pip", "install", "--python", str(python_path), str(install_target))
188	
189	    requirements_file = _metadata_path(executor, "requirements_file")
190	    if requirements_file is not None:
191	        return ("uv", "pip", "install", "--python", str(python_path), "-r", str(requirements_file))
192	
193	    if executor.isolation.requirements:
194	        return ("uv", "pip", "install", "--python", str(python_path), *executor.isolation.requirements)
195	    return None
196	
197	
198	def _metadata_path(executor: ExecutorDefinition, key: str) -> Path | None:
199	    raw = executor.metadata.get(key)
200	    if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw:
201	        return None
202	    return Path(raw).expanduser().resolve()
203	
204	
205	def _safe_executor_id(executor_id: str) -> str:
206	    return re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]+", "_", executor_id)
207	
208	
209	def _executor_install_id(executor: ExecutorDefinition) -> str:
210	    package_id = executor.metadata.get("package_id")
211	    if isinstance(package_id, str) and package_id:
212	        return package_id
213	    return executor.id
214	
215	
216	def _validate_git_manifest_source(source: _GitManifestSource, *, label: str) -> None:
217	    refs = [source.commit_sha, source.tag, source.branch, source.source_ref]
218	    if sum(1 for ref in refs if ref) != 1:
219	        raise ExecutorInstallError(f"git {label} source must specify exactly one of commit_sha, tag, branch, or source_ref")
220	    if not source.repo_url.strip():
221	        raise ExecutorInstallError(f"git {label} source repo_url is required")
222	    _safe_relative_path(source.manifest_path, "manifest_path")
223	    if source.install_subdir:
224	        _safe_relative_path(source.install_subdir, "install_subdir")
225	
226	
227	def _safe_manifest_source_dir(source: _GitManifestSource) -> Path:
228	    ref = source.commit_sha or source.tag or source.branch or source.source_ref or ""
229	    digest = sha256(f"{source.repo_url}\n{ref}\n{source.manifest_path}".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]
230	    return Path(_safe_executor_id(source.expected_id)) / digest / "repo"
231	
232	
233	def _clone_git_manifest_source(source: _GitManifestSource, checkout: Path) -> None:
234	    checkout.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
235	    if source.commit_sha:
236	        _run_git(("git", "clone", "--filter=blob:none", source.repo_url, str(checkout)))
237	        _checkout_git_manifest_ref(source, checkout)
238	        return
239	    ref = source.tag or source.branch or source.source_ref
240	    assert ref is not None
241	    _run_git(("git", "clone", "--depth", "1", "--branch", ref, source.repo_url, str(checkout)))
242	
243	
244	def _checkout_git_manifest_ref(source: _GitManifestSource, checkout: Path) -> None:
245	    ref = source.commit_sha or source.tag or source.branch or source.source_ref
246	    assert ref is not None
247	    _run_git(("git", "-C", str(checkout), "checkout", "--detach", ref))
248	
249	
250	def _run_git(command: tuple[str, ...]) -> None:
251	    completed = subprocess.run(list(command), check=False, capture_output=True, text=True)
252	    if completed.returncode != 0:
253	        detail = (completed.stderr or completed.stdout).strip()
254	        suffix = f": {detail}" if detail else ""
255	        raise ExecutorInstallError(f"command failed: {' '.join(command)}{suffix}")
256	
257	
258	def _safe_child_path(root: Path, relative: str) -> Path:
259	    relative_path = _safe_relative_path(relative, "manifest_path")
260	    child = (root / relative_path).resolve()
261	    root_resolved = root.resolve()
262	    if root_resolved != child and root_resolved not in child.parents:
263	        raise ExecutorInstallError("manifest_path escapes git checkout")
264	    return child
265	
266	
267	def _safe_relative_path(value: str, label: str) -> Path:
268	    path = Path(value)
269	    if path.is_absolute() or ".." in path.parts or not value.strip():
270	        raise ExecutorInstallError(f"{label} must be a non-empty relative path")
271	    return path
272	
273
```

```
18	BUILTIN_STEP_ORDER: tuple[str, ...] = (
19	    "transcribe",

37	class ExecutorRegistryError(ExecutorValidationError):
38	    """Raised when a executor registry is inconsistent."""
39	
40	
41	class ExecutorRegistry:
42	    """Small in-memory registry keyed by executor id."""
43	
44	    def __init__(self, executors: Iterable[ExecutorDefinition | dict[str, Any]] = ()) -> None:
45	        self._executors: dict[str, ExecutorDefinition] = {}
46	        for executor in executors:
47	            self.register(executor)
48	
49	    def register(self, executor: ExecutorDefinition | dict[str, Any]) -> ExecutorDefinition:
50	        definition = validate_executor_definition(executor)
51	        if definition.id in self._executors:
52	            raise ExecutorRegistryError(f"duplicate executor id {definition.id!r}")
53	        self._executors[definition.id] = definition
54	        return definition
55	
56	    def get(self, executor_id: str) -> ExecutorDefinition:
57	        try:
58	            return self._executors[executor_id]
59	        except KeyError as exc:
60	            raise KeyError(f"unknown executor id {executor_id!r}") from exc
61	
62	    def list(self, kind: str | None = None) -> tuple[ExecutorDefinition, ...]:
63	        if kind is not None and kind not in {"built_in", "external"}:
64	            raise ExecutorRegistryError("kind must be one of ['built_in', 'external']")
65	        executors = self._executors.values()
66	        if kind is not None:
67	            executors = [executor for executor in executors if executor.kind == kind]
68	        return tuple(sorted(executors, key=lambda executor: executor.id))
69	
70	    def validate_all(self) -> tuple[ExecutorDefinition, ...]:
71	        for executor in self._executors.values():
72	            validate_executor_definition(executor)
73	        self._validate_graph_references()
74	        return self.list()
75	
76	    def to_dict(self, kind: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
77	        return {"executors": [executor.to_dict() for executor in self.list(kind=kind)]}
78	
79	    def to_json(self, *, kind: str | None = None, indent: int | None = 2) -> str:
80	        return json.dumps(self.to_dict(kind=kind), indent=indent, sort_keys=True)
81	
82	    def as_mapping(self) -> MappingProxyType[str, ExecutorDefinition]:
83	        return MappingProxyType(dict(self._executors))
84	
85	    def _validate_graph_references(self) -> None:
86	        known_ids = set(self._executors)
87	        for executor in self._executors.values():
88	            for dependency in executor.graph.depends_on:
89	                if dependency not in known_ids:
90	                    raise ExecutorRegistryError(f"executor {executor.id!r} depends on unknown executor {dependency!r}")
91	                if dependency == executor.id:
92	                    raise ExecutorRegistryError(f"executor {executor.id!r} cannot depend on itself")
93	
94	
95	def executor_id_for_step(step_name: str) -> str:
96	    return f"builtin.{step_name}"
97	
98	
99	def load_builtin_executors() -> tuple[ExecutorDefinition, ...]:
100	    folder_executors: list[ExecutorDefinition] = []
101	    expected_ids = {executor_id_for_step(step_name) for step_name in BUILTIN_STEP_ORDER}
102	    for path in _builtin_folder_roots():
103	        folder_executors.extend(load_folder_executors(path))
104	    by_id = {executor.id: executor for executor in folder_executors if executor.id in expected_ids}
105	    missing = [executor_id_for_step(step_name) for step_name in BUILTIN_STEP_ORDER if executor_id_for_step(step_name) not in by_id]
106	    if missing:
107	        raise ExecutorRegistryError(f"missing built-in executor folder metadata: {', '.join(missing)}")
108	    return tuple(by_id[executor_id_for_step(step_name)] for step_name in BUILTIN_STEP_ORDER)
109	
110	
111	def load_curated_executors() -> tuple[ExecutorDefinition, ...]:
112	    executors: list[ExecutorDefinition] = []
113	    for path in _curated_manifest_paths():
114	        executors.append(load_executor_manifest(path))
115	    for path in _curated_folder_roots():
116	        executors.extend(load_folder_executors(path))
117	    return tuple(executors)
118	
119	
120	def load_bundled_executors() -> tuple[ExecutorDefinition, ...]:
121	    executors: list[ExecutorDefinition] = []
122	    for path in _bundled_manifest_paths():
123	        executors.append(load_executor_manifest(path))
124	    for path in _bundled_folder_roots():
125	        executors.extend(load_folder_executors(path))
126	    return tuple(executors)
127	
128	
129	def load_default_registry(banodoco_config: BanodocoCatalogConfig | None = None) -> ExecutorRegistry:
130	    registry = ExecutorRegistry()
131	    for executor in load_builtin_executors():
132	        registry.register(executor)
133	    for executor in load_bundled_executors():
134	        registry.register(executor)
135	    for executor in load_curated_executors():
136	        registry.register(executor)
137	    for executor in load_project_executors():
138	        if executor.id not in registry.as_mapping():
139	            registry.register(executor)
140	    if banodoco_config is not None and banodoco_config.enabled:
141	        for executor in load_banodoco_catalog_executors(banodoco_config):
142	            registry.register(executor)
143	    registry.validate_all()
144	    return registry
145	
146	
147	def load_project_executors() -> tuple[ExecutorDefinition, ...]:
148	    executors: list[ExecutorDefinition] = []
149	    for path in _project_folder_roots():
150	        executors.extend(load_folder_executors(path))
```

```
1	"""Local-only install planning for element dependencies."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import re
6	import shlex
7	import subprocess
8	from dataclasses import dataclass
9	from pathlib import Path
10	
11	from artagents._paths import REPO_ROOT
12	
13	from .schema import ElementDefinition
14	
15	
16	class ElementInstallError(RuntimeError):
17	    """Raised when an element dependency install fails."""
18	
19	
20	@dataclass(frozen=True)
21	class ElementInstallPlan:
22	    element_id: str
23	    root: Path
24	    venv_path: Path | None
25	    node_prefix: Path | None
26	    commands: tuple[tuple[str, ...], ...]
27	    noop_reason: str | None = None
28	
29	    def command_lines(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
30	        return tuple(shlex.join(command) for command in self.commands)
31	
32	
33	@dataclass(frozen=True)
34	class ElementInstallResult:
35	    plan: ElementInstallPlan
36	    returncode: int = 0
37	
38	
39	def safe_element_install_id(element: ElementDefinition) -> str:
40	    raw = f"{element.kind}-{element.id}"
41	    return re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]+", "-", raw).strip("-") or "element"
42	
43	
44	def build_element_install_plan(
45	    element: ElementDefinition,
46	    *,
47	    project_root: str | Path = REPO_ROOT,
48	) -> ElementInstallPlan:
49	    install_root = Path(project_root) / ".artagents" / "elements" / safe_element_install_id(element)
50	    commands: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []
51	    venv_path: Path | None = None
52	    node_prefix: Path | None = None
53	    if element.dependencies.python_requirements:
54	        venv_path = install_root / "venv"
55	        python_path = venv_path / "bin" / "python"
56	        commands.append(("uv", "venv", str(venv_path)))
57	        commands.append(("uv", "pip", "install", "--python", str(python_path), *element.dependencies.python_requirements))
58	    if element.dependencies.js_packages:
59	        node_prefix = install_root / "node"
60	        commands.append(("npm", "install", "--prefix", str(node_prefix), *element.dependencies.js_packages))
61	    if not commands:
62	        return ElementInstallPlan(
63	            element_id=element.id,
64	            root=install_root,
65	            venv_path=venv_path,
66	            node_prefix=node_prefix,
67	            commands=(),
68	            noop_reason="no dependencies declared",
69	        )
70	    return ElementInstallPlan(
71	        element_id=element.id,
72	        root=install_root,
73	        venv_path=venv_path,
74	        node_prefix=node_prefix,
75	        commands=tuple(commands),
76	    )
77	
78	
79	def install_element(
80	    element: ElementDefinition,
81	    *,
82	    project_root: str | Path = REPO_ROOT,
83	    dry_run: bool = True,
84	) -> ElementInstallResult:
85	    plan = build_element_install_plan(element, project_root=project_root)
86	    if dry_run or plan.noop_reason:
87	        return ElementInstallResult(plan=plan, returncode=0)
88	    plan.root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
89	    for command in plan.commands:
90	        completed = subprocess.run(command)
91	        if completed.returncode:
92	            raise ElementInstallError(f"element install command failed with {completed.returncode}: {shlex.join(command)}")
93	    return ElementInstallResult(plan=plan, returncode=0)
94
```

```
64	def test_lock_timeout_guidance_avoids_thread_gc(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
65	    store = ThreadIndexStore(tmp_path, lock_timeout=0.15)
66	    store.state_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
67	    with store.lock_path.open("a+", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
68	        fcntl.flock(handle.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX)
69	        started = time.monotonic()
70	        with pytest.raises(ThreadIndexLockTimeout) as raised:
71	            store.read()
72	        elapsed = time.monotonic() - started
73	        fcntl.flock(handle.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_UN)
74	
75	    message = str(raised.value)
76	    assert elapsed >= 0.10
77	    assert "threads.json lock" in message
78	    assert "thread gc" not in message

105	    store = ThreadIndexStore(tmp_path)

108	    index["active_thread_id"] = thread_id

110	
111	    run_ids = [generate_run_id() for _ in range(8)]
112	    processes = [
113	        multiprocessing.Process(target=_append_run_id, args=(tmp_path, thread_id, run_id))
114	        for run_id in run_ids
115	    ]
116	    for process in processes:
117	        process.start()
118	    for process in processes:
119	        process.join(timeout=10)

122	    final_index = store.read()
```
