48	    plan: ExecutorInstallPlan
49	    dry_run: bool = False
50	    returncode: int = 0
51	
52	
53	def executor_environment_path(executor: ExecutorDefinition) -> Path:
54	    """Return the deterministic repo-local virtualenv path for a executor."""
55	
56	    return REPO_ROOT / ".artagents" / "executors" / _safe_executor_id(_executor_install_id(executor)) / "venv"
57	
58	
59	def executor_python_path(executor: ExecutorDefinition) -> Path:
60	    """Return the Python executable path inside a executor virtualenv."""
61	
62	    env_path = executor_environment_path(executor)
63	    if sys.platform == "win32":
64	        return env_path / "Scripts" / "python.exe"
65	    return env_path / "bin" / "python"
66	
67	
68	def build_executor_install_plan(executor: ExecutorDefinition) -> ExecutorInstallPlan:
69	    """Build uv commands for explicit executor installation."""
70	
71	    if executor.kind == "built_in":
72	        return ExecutorInstallPlan(
73	            executor_id=executor.id,
74	            kind=executor.kind,
75	            environment_path=None,
76	            python_path=None,
77	            noop_reason="built-in executors use the host Python environment",
78	        )
79	    if executor.kind != "external":
80	        raise ExecutorInstallError(f"unsupported executor kind {executor.kind!r}")
81	
82	    env_path = executor_environment_path(executor)
83	    python_path = executor_python_path(executor)
84	    commands: list[tuple[str, ...]] = [("uv", "venv", str(env_path))]
85	    dependency_command = _dependency_install_command(executor, python_path)
86	    if dependency_command is not None:
87	        commands.append(dependency_command)
88	    return ExecutorInstallPlan(
89	        executor_id=executor.id,
90	        kind=executor.kind,
91	        environment_path=env_path,
92	        python_path=python_path,
93	        commands=tuple(commands),
94	    )
95	
96	
97	def install_executor(executor: ExecutorDefinition, *, dry_run: bool = False) -> ExecutorInstallResult:
98	    """Install dependencies for a executor, or return the dry-run plan."""
99	
100	    plan = build_executor_install_plan(executor)
101	    if dry_run or not plan.commands:
102	        return ExecutorInstallResult(plan=plan, dry_run=dry_run)
103	    for command in plan.commands:
104	        completed = subprocess.run(list(command), check=False)
105	        if completed.returncode != 0:
106	            return ExecutorInstallResult(plan=plan, dry_run=False, returncode=completed.returncode)
107	    return ExecutorInstallResult(plan=plan, dry_run=False)
108	
109	
110	def fetch_git_executor_manifest(
111	    source: GitExecutorSource,
112	    *,
113	    cache_dir: Path | None = None,
114	    refresh: bool = False,
115	) -> dict:
116	    """Fetch a git-backed executor manifest and verify its catalog identity."""
117	
118	    return _fetch_git_manifest(
119	        _GitManifestSource(
120	            repo_url=source.repo_url,
121	            manifest_path=source.manifest_path,
122	            expected_id=source.expected_executor_id,
123	            commit_sha=source.commit_sha,
124	            tag=source.tag,
125	            branch=source.branch,
126	            source_ref=source.source_ref,
127	            install_subdir=source.install_subdir,
128	        ),
129	        cache_root=cache_dir or (REPO_ROOT / ".artagents" / "banodoco-executors"),
130	        refresh=refresh,
131	        label="executor",
132	    )
133	
134	
135	@dataclass(frozen=True)
136	class _GitManifestSource:
137	    repo_url: str
138	    manifest_path: str
139	    expected_id: str
140	    commit_sha: str | None = None
141	    tag: str | None = None
142	    branch: str | None = None
143	    source_ref: str | None = None
144	    install_subdir: str | None = None
145	
146	
147	def _fetch_git_manifest(
148	    source: _GitManifestSource,
149	    *,
150	    cache_root: Path,
151	    refresh: bool,
152	    label: str,
153	) -> dict:
154	    _validate_git_manifest_source(source, label=label)
155	    checkout = cache_root / _safe_manifest_source_dir(source)
156	    if refresh and checkout.exists():
157	        shutil.rmtree(checkout.parent)
158	    checkout.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
159	
160	    if not checkout.exists():
161	        _clone_git_manifest_source(source, checkout)
162	    elif refresh:
163	        _run_git(("git", "-C", str(checkout), "fetch", "--tags", "--prune"))
164	        _checkout_git_manifest_ref(source, checkout)
165	
166	    manifest_path = _safe_child_path(checkout, source.manifest_path)
167	    if not manifest_path.is_file():
168	        raise ExecutorInstallError(f"git {label} manifest not found: {manifest_path}")
169	    try:
170	        manifest = loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
171	    except ValueError as exc:
172	        raise ExecutorInstallError(f"invalid git {label} manifest JSON: {manifest_path}") from exc
173	    if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
174	        raise ExecutorInstallError(f"git {label} manifest must be a JSON object")
175	    manifest_id = manifest.get("id")
176	    if manifest_id != source.expected_id:
177	        raise ExecutorInstallError(
178	            f"git {label} identity mismatch: expected {source.expected_id!r}, fetched {manifest_id!r}"
179	        )
180	    return manifest
181	
182	
183	def _dependency_install_command(executor: ExecutorDefinition, python_path: Path) -> tuple[str, ...] | None:
184	    pyproject_file = _metadata_path(executor, "pyproject_file")
185	    if pyproject_file is not None:
186	        install_target = _metadata_path(executor, "executor_root") or pyproject_file.parent
187	        return ("uv", "pip", "install", "--python", str(python_path), str(install_target))
188	
189	    requirements_file = _metadata_path(executor, "requirements_file")
190	    if requirements_file is not None:
191	        return ("uv", "pip", "install", "--python", str(python_path), "-r", str(requirements_file))
192	
193	    if executor.isolation.requirements:
194	        return ("uv", "pip", "install", "--python", str(python_path), *executor.isolation.requirements)
195	    return None
196	
197	
198	def _metadata_path(executor: ExecutorDefinition, key: str) -> Path | None:
199	    raw = executor.metadata.get(key)
200	    if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw:
201	        return None
202	    return Path(raw).expanduser().resolve()
203	
204	
205	def _safe_executor_id(executor_id: str) -> str:
206	    return re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]+", "_", executor_id)
207	
208	
209	def _executor_install_id(executor: ExecutorDefinition) -> str:
210	    package_id = executor.metadata.get("package_id")
211	    if isinstance(package_id, str) and package_id:
212	        return package_id
213	    return executor.id
214	
215	
216	def _validate_git_manifest_source(source: _GitManifestSource, *, label: str) -> None:
217	    refs = [source.commit_sha, source.tag, source.branch, source.source_ref]
218	    if sum(1 for ref in refs if ref) != 1:
219	        raise ExecutorInstallError(f"git {label} source must specify exactly one of commit_sha, tag, branch, or source_ref")
220	    if not source.repo_url.strip():
221	        raise ExecutorInstallError(f"git {label} source repo_url is required")
222	    _safe_relative_path(source.manifest_path, "manifest_path")
223	    if source.install_subdir:
224	        _safe_relative_path(source.install_subdir, "install_subdir")
225	
226	
227	def _safe_manifest_source_dir(source: _GitManifestSource) -> Path:
228	    ref = source.commit_sha or source.tag or source.branch or source.source_ref or ""
229	    digest = sha256(f"{source.repo_url}\n{ref}\n{source.manifest_path}".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]
230	    return Path(_safe_executor_id(source.expected_id)) / digest / "repo"
231	
232	
233	def _clone_git_manifest_source(source: _GitManifestSource, checkout: Path) -> None:
234	    checkout.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
235	    if source.commit_sha:
236	        _run_git(("git", "clone", "--filter=blob:none", source.repo_url, str(checkout)))
237	        _checkout_git_manifest_ref(source, checkout)
238	        return
239	    ref = source.tag or source.branch or source.source_ref
240	    assert ref is not None
241	    _run_git(("git", "clone", "--depth", "1", "--branch", ref, source.repo_url, str(checkout)))
242	
243	
244	def _checkout_git_manifest_ref(source: _GitManifestSource, checkout: Path) -> None:
245	    ref = source.commit_sha or source.tag or source.branch or source.source_ref
246	    assert ref is not None
247	    _run_git(("git", "-C", str(checkout), "checkout", "--detach", ref))
248	
249	
250	def _run_git(command: tuple[str, ...]) -> None:
251	    completed = subprocess.run(list(command), check=False, capture_output=True, text=True)
252	    if completed.returncode != 0:
253	        detail = (completed.stderr or completed.stdout).strip()
254	        suffix = f": {detail}" if detail else ""
255	        raise ExecutorInstallError(f"command failed: {' '.join(command)}{suffix}")
256	
257	
258	def _safe_child_path(root: Path, relative: str) -> Path:
259	    relative_path = _safe_relative_path(relative, "manifest_path")
260	    child = (root / relative_path).resolve()
261	    root_resolved = root.resolve()
262	    if root_resolved != child and root_resolved not in child.parents:
263	        raise ExecutorInstallError("manifest_path escapes git checkout")
264	    return child
265	
266	
267	def _safe_relative_path(value: str, label: str) -> Path:
268	    path = Path(value)
269	    if path.is_absolute() or ".." in path.parts or not value.strip():
270	        raise ExecutorInstallError(f"{label} must be a non-empty relative path")
271	    return path
272	
273	
274	__all__ = [
275	    "ExecutorInstallError",
276	    "GitExecutorSource",
277	    "ExecutorInstallPlan",
278	    "ExecutorInstallResult",
279	    "build_executor_install_plan",
280	    "fetch_git_executor_manifest",
281	    "install_executor",
282	    "executor_environment_path",
283	    "executor_python_path",
284	]
285

80	def run_executor(request: ExecutorRunRequest, registry: ExecutorRegistry | None = None) -> ExecutorRunResult:
81	    active_registry = registry or load_default_registry()
82	    executor = active_registry.get(request.executor_id)
83	    context = thread_wrapper.begin_executor_run(request, executor)
84	    try:

86	    except Exception as exc:

88	        raise
89	    thread_wrapper.finalize_result(context, result)
90	    return result

93	def _run_executor_inner(request: ExecutorRunRequest, executor: ExecutorDefinition) -> ExecutorRunResult:

115	            dry_run=request.dry_run,

219	def build_executor_command(request: ExecutorRunRequest, registry: ExecutorRegistry | None = None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
220	    active_registry = registry or load_default_registry()
221	    executor = active_registry.get(request.executor_id)
222	    values = _request_values(request)
223	    _validate_required_inputs(executor, values)
224	    condition_result = evaluate_conditions(executor, values)
225	    if condition_result.skipped:
226	        return ()
227	    if executor.kind == "built_in" and "pipeline_step" in executor.metadata:
228	        step = _step_for_executor(executor)
229	        args = build_pipeline_context(request, executor)

234	def _run_builtin_executor(executor: ExecutorDefinition, request: ExecutorRunRequest) -> ExecutorRunResult:
235	    pipeline = _pipeline_module()
236	    step = _step_for_executor(executor)
237	    args = build_pipeline_context(request, executor)
238	    command = tuple(step.build_cmd(args))
239	    if request.dry_run:
240	        return ExecutorRunResult(
241	            executor_id=executor.id,
242	            kind=executor.kind,
243	            command=command,
244	            payload={"executor_id": executor.id, "missing_binaries": [], "returncode": None, "skipped": False, "skipped_reason": ""},
245	            dry_run=True,
246	        )
247	    if args.brief.exists():
248	        pipeline.prepare_brief_artifacts(args)
249	    returncode = pipeline.run_step(step, list(command), args)
250	    return ExecutorRunResult(
251	        executor_id=executor.id,
252	        kind=executor.kind,
253	        command=command,
254	        payload={"executor_id": executor.id, "missing_binaries": [], "returncode": returncode, "skipped": False, "skipped_reason": ""},
255	        returncode=returncode,
256	    )

259	def _run_external_executor(executor: ExecutorDefinition, request: ExecutorRunRequest, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> ExecutorRunResult:
260	    command, cwd, env = _expand_external_command(executor, request, values)
261	    if request.dry_run:
262	        return ExecutorRunResult(
263	            executor_id=executor.id,
264	            kind=executor.kind,
265	            command=command,
266	            cwd=cwd,
267	            env=env,
268	            payload={"executor_id": executor.id, "missing_binaries": [], "returncode": None, "skipped": False, "skipped_reason": ""},
269	            dry_run=True,
270	        )
271	    completed = subprocess.run(
272	        list(command),
273	        cwd=cwd,
274	        env={**os.environ, **env, **thread_wrapper.subprocess_env()},
275	        check=False,
276	    )
277	    return ExecutorRunResult(
278	        executor_id=executor.id,
279	        kind=executor.kind,
280	        command=command,
281	        cwd=cwd,
282	        env=env,
283	        payload={
284	            "executor_id": executor.id,
285	            "missing_binaries": [],
286	            "returncode": completed.returncode,
287	            "skipped": False,
288	            "skipped_reason": "",
289	        },
290	        returncode=completed.returncode,
291	    )
292	
293	
294	def _expand_external_command(
295	    executor: ExecutorDefinition,
296	    request: ExecutorRunRequest,
297	    values: Mapping[str, Any],
298	) -> tuple[tuple[str, ...], str | None, dict[str, str]]:
299	    if executor.command is None:
300	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"executor {executor.id!r} has no command")
301	    placeholders = _placeholder_values(executor, request, values)
302	    argv = tuple(_expand_placeholders(part, placeholders) for part in executor.command.argv)
303	    cwd = _expand_placeholders(executor.command.cwd, placeholders) if executor.command.cwd else None
304	    env = {[REDACTED](value, placeholders) for key, value in executor.command.env.items()}
305	    return argv, cwd, env
306	
307	
308	def _placeholder_values(executor: ExecutorDefinition, request: ExecutorRunRequest, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, str]:
309	    out = Path(request.out).expanduser().resolve()
310	    placeholders: dict[str, str] = {
311	        "out": str(out),
312	    }
313	    python_exec = _resolve_python_exec(executor, request, values)
314	    if python_exec is not None:
315	        placeholders["python_exec"] = python_exec
316	    brief = values.get("brief") or request.brief
317	    if brief is not None:
318	        brief_path = Path(str(brief)).expanduser().resolve()
319	        placeholders["brief"] = str(brief_path)
320	        brief_slug = str(values.get("brief_slug") or _default_brief_slug(brief_path, out))
321	        brief_out = out / "briefs" / brief_slug
322	        placeholders["brief_slug"] = brief_slug
323	        placeholders["brief_out"] = str(brief_out)
324	        placeholders["brief_copy"] = str(brief_out / "brief.txt")
325	    for key, value in values.items():
326	        if value is None:
327	            continue
328	        placeholders[key] = _stringify_value(value)
329	    for output in executor.outputs:
330	        output_path = _output_value(output, request, placeholders)
331	        placeholders[output.name] = output_path
332	        if output.placeholder:
333	            placeholders[output.placeholder] = output_path
334	    return placeholders
335	
336	
337	def _output_value(output: ExecutorOutput, request: ExecutorRunRequest, placeholders: Mapping[str, str]) -> str:
338	    if output.name in request.outputs:
339	        return _stringify_value(request.outputs[output.name])
340	    if output.placeholder and output.placeholder in request.outputs:
341	        return _stringify_value(request.outputs[output.placeholder])
342	    if output.path_template:
343	        return _expand_placeholders(output.path_template, placeholders)
344	    return str((Path(request.out).expanduser().resolve() / output.name).resolve())
345	
346	
347	def _resolve_python_exec(executor: ExecutorDefinition, request: ExecutorRunRequest, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str | None:
348	    input_override = values.get("python_exec")
349	    if _has_value(input_override):
350	        return str(input_override)
351	    if _has_value(request.python_exec):
352	        return str(request.python_exec)
353	    if not _executor_uses_placeholder(executor, "python_exec"):
354	        return None
355	    if executor.kind == "external" and executor.isolation.mode == "subprocess":
356	        installed_python = executor_python_path(executor)
357	        if installed_python.is_file():
358	            return str(installed_python)
359	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(
360	            f"executor {executor.id!r} requires an installed Python environment; "
361	            f"run `python3 -m artagents executors install {executor.id}` or pass python_exec as an input override"
362	        )
363	    return sys.executable
364	
365	
366	def _executor_uses_placeholder(executor: ExecutorDefinition, placeholder: str) -> bool:
367	    if executor.command is None:
368	        return False
369	    needle = f"{{{placeholder}}}"
370	    if any(needle in part for part in executor.command.argv):
371	        return True
372	    if executor.command.cwd and needle in executor.command.cwd:
373	        return True
374	    return any(needle in value for value in executor.command.env.values())
375	
376	
377	def _expand_placeholders(value: str, placeholders: Mapping[str, str]) -> str:
378	    def replace(match: re.Match[str]) -> str:
379	        key = match.group(1)
380	        if key not in placeholders:
381	            raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"missing value for placeholder {{{key}}}")
382	        return placeholders[key]
383	
384	    return _PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(replace, value)
385	
386	
387	def _validate_required_inputs(executor: ExecutorDefinition, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
388	    missing = [
389	        port.name
390	        for port in executor.inputs
391	        if port.required and port.default is None and not _has_value(values.get(port.name))
392	    ]
393	    if missing:
394	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"executor {executor.id!r} missing required input(s): {', '.join(missing)}")
395	
396	
397	def _evaluate_condition(condition: ConditionSpec, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> ConditionResult:
398	    if condition.kind == "always":
399	        return ConditionResult()
400	    if condition.kind == "requires_input":
401	        if not condition.input or not _has_value(values.get(condition.input)):
402	            raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"condition requires input {condition.input!r}")
403	        return ConditionResult()
404	    if condition.kind == "requires_file":
405	        candidate = values.get(condition.input) if condition.input else condition.path
406	        if not _has_value(candidate):
407	            raise ExecutorRunnerError("condition requires a file path")
408	        path = Path(str(candidate)).expanduser()
409	        if not path.is_file():
410	            raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"condition requires file: {path}")
411	        return ConditionResult()
412	    if condition.kind == "skip_if_input" and condition.input and _has_value(values.get(condition.input)):
413	        return ConditionResult(skipped=True, reason=f"input {condition.input!r} is set")
414	    raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"unsupported condition kind {condition.kind!r}")
415	
416	
417	def _step_for_executor(executor: ExecutorDefinition) -> Any:
418	    step_name = executor.metadata.get("pipeline_step")
419	    if not isinstance(step_name, str):
420	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"built-in executor {executor.id!r} is missing metadata.pipeline_step")
421	    steps = _builtin_steps_by_name()
422	    if step_name not in steps:
423	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"built-in executor {executor.id!r} references unknown pipeline step {step_name!r}")
424	    return steps[step_name]
425	
426	
427	def _request_values(request: ExecutorRunRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
428	    values = dict(request.inputs)
429	    if request.brief is not None and "brief" not in values:
430	        values["brief"] = request.brief
431	    if request.python_exec is not None and "python_exec" not in values:
432	        values["python_exec"] = request.python_exec
433	    values.setdefault("verbose", request.verbose)
434	    return values
435	
436	
437	def _has_value(value: Any) -> bool:
438	    return value is not None and value != ""
439	
440	
441	def _optional_path(value: Any) -> Path | None:
442	    if value is None or value == "":
443	        return None
444	    return Path(str(value)).expanduser().resolve()
445	
446	
447	def _optional_asset_path(value: Any) -> Path | str | None:
448	    if value is None or value == "":
449	        return None
450	    text = str(value)
451	    pipeline = _pipeline_module()
452	    if pipeline.asset_cache.is_url(text):
453	        return text
454	    return Path(text).expanduser().resolve()
455	
456	
457	def _optional_float(value: Any) -> float | None:
458	    if value is None or value == "":
459	        return None
460	    return float(value)
461	
462	
463	def _as_string_list(value: Any) -> list[str]:
464	    if value is None or value == "":
465	        return []
466	    if isinstance(value, str):
467	        return [value]
468	    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
469	        return [str(item) for item in value]
470	    return [str(value)]
471	
472	
473	def _parse_asset_pairs(values: list[str]) -> list[tuple[str, Path | str]]:
474	    pairs: list[tuple[str, Path | str]] = []
475	    for raw in values:
476	        if "=" not in raw:
477	            raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"invalid asset value {raw!r}; expected KEY=PATH")
478	        key, path_text = raw.split("=", 1)
479	        key = key.strip()
480	        path_text = path_text.strip()
481	        if not key or not path_text:
482	            raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"invalid asset value {raw!r}; expected KEY=PATH")
483	        pipeline = _pipeline_module()
484	        if pipeline.asset_cache.is_url(path_text):
485	            pairs.append((key, path_text))
486	        else:
487	            pairs.append((key, Path(path_text).expanduser().resolve()))
488	    return pairs
489	
490	
491	def _normalize_extra_args(value: Any) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
492	    if value is None:
493	        return {}
494	    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
495	        raise ExecutorRunnerError("extra_args must be an object keyed by step name")
496	    return {str(key): _as_string_list(raw_values) for key, raw_values in value.items()}
497	
498	
499	def _default_brief_slug(brief: Path, out: Path) -> str:
500	    generic_brief_names = {"brief", "plan", "prompt"}
501	    return out.name if brief.stem.lower() in generic_brief_names else brief.stem
502	
503	
504	def _stringify_value(value: Any) -> str:
505	    if isinstance(value, Path):
506	        return str(value)
507	    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
508	        return ",".join(str(item) for item in value)
509	    return str(value)
510	
511	
512	def _required_input(inputs: Mapping[str, Any], key: str) -> str:
513	    value = inputs.get(key)
514	    if value in (None, ""):
515	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"{key} is required")
516	    return str(value)
517	
518	
519	def _optional_input(inputs: Mapping[str, Any], key: str) -> Any:
520	    value = inputs.get(key)
521	    if value in (None, ""):
522	        return None
523	    return value
524	
525	
526	__all__ = [
527	    "ConditionResult",
528	    "ExecutorRunRequest",
529	    "ExecutorRunResult",
530	    "ExecutorRunnerError",
531	    "build_pipeline_context",
532	    "build_executor_command",
533	    "check_executor_binaries",
534	    "evaluate_conditions",
535	    "run_executor",
536	]
537

17	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
18	    parser = build_parser()
19	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
20	    try:
21	        registry = load_default_registry(_banodoco_config_from_args(args))
22	        return int(args.handler(args, registry))
23	    except (KeyError, ExecutorValidationError, ValueError) as exc:
24	        print(f"executors: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
25	        return 2
26	
27	
28	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
29	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
30	        prog="python3 -m artagents executors",
31	        description="List, inspect, validate, install, and run ArtAgents executors.",
32	        formatter_class=argparse.RawTextHelpFormatter,
33	    )
34	    parser.add_argument("--banodoco-agent-executors", action="store_true", help="Opt in to loading executors from the Banodoco website catalog.")
35	    parser.add_argument("--banodoco-catalog-url", help="Banodoco website agent-executor catalog Edge Function URL.")
36	    parser.add_argument("--banodoco-cache-dir", help="Cache directory for git-backed Banodoco executors.")
37	    parser.add_argument("--banodoco-refresh", action="store_true", help="Refresh cached git checkouts before loading Banodoco executors.")
38	    parser.add_argument("--no-banodoco-defaults", action="store_true", help="Skip Banodoco catalog executors marked default.")
39	    parser.add_argument("--no-banodoco-mandatory", action="store_true", help="Skip Banodoco catalog executors marked mandatory.")
40	    subparsers = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
41	
42	    list_parser = subparsers.add_parser("list", help="List available executors.")
43	    list_parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Emit machine-readable JSON.")
44	    list_parser.add_argument("--kind", choices=("built_in", "external"), help="Filter executors by kind.")
45	    list_parser.set_defaults(handler=_cmd_list)
46	
47	    inspect_parser = subparsers.add_parser("inspect", help="Inspect one executor.")
48	    inspect_parser.add_argument("executor_id")
49	    inspect_parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Emit machine-readable JSON.")
50	    inspect_parser.set_defaults(handler=_cmd_inspect)
51	
52	    validate_parser = subparsers.add_parser("validate", help="Validate executor metadata.")
53	    validate_parser.add_argument("executor_id", nargs="?")
54	    validate_parser.add_argument("--check-binaries", action="store_true", help="Also require declared external binaries to be on PATH.")
55	    validate_parser.set_defaults(handler=_cmd_validate)
56	
57	    install_parser = subparsers.add_parser("install", help="Install dependencies for one executor.")
58	    install_parser.add_argument("executor_id")
59	    install_parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Print install commands without executing them.")
60	    install_parser.set_defaults(handler=_cmd_install)
61	
62	    run_parser = subparsers.add_parser("run", help="Run or dry-run one executor.")
63	    run_parser.add_argument("executor_id")
64	    run_parser.add_argument("--out", help="Output directory for runtime placeholders.")
65	    run_parser.add_argument("--input", action="append", default=[], metavar="NAME=VALUE", help="Executor input value; may be repeated.")
66	    run_parser.add_argument("--brief", help="Brief path for built-in pipeline context synthesis.")
67	    run_parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Build and print the command without executing it.")
68	    run_parser.add_argument("--check-binaries", action="store_true", help="Also require declared external binaries to be on PATH.")
69	    run_parser.add_argument("--python-exec", help="Python executable for {python_exec} placeholders.")
70	    run_parser.add_argument("--verbose", action="store_true", help="Stream subprocess output for built-in pipeline steps.")
71	    run_parser.add_argument("--thread", help="Thread id, @new, or @none for this run.")
72	    run_parser.add_argument("--variants", type=int, help="Request a sibling variant count for variant-aware producers.")
73	    run_parser.add_argument("--from", dest="from_ref", help="Consume a specific prior run or variant, e.g. <run-id>:<n>.")

79	    run_parser.add_argument("--privacy-status", default=None, help="YouTube privacy status: private, unlisted, or public.")
80	    run_parser.add_argument("--playlist-id", help="Optional YouTube playlist ID.")
81	    run_parser.add_argument("--made-for-kids", action="store_true", help="Mark the video as made for kids.")
82	    run_parser.set_defaults(handler=_cmd_run)
83	    return parser

86	def _banodoco_config_from_args(args: argparse.Namespace) -> BanodocoCatalogConfig:
87	    env_config = BanodocoCatalogConfig.from_env()
88	    enabled = bool(args.banodoco_agent_executors or env_config.enabled)
89	    return BanodocoCatalogConfig(

91	        catalog_url=args.banodoco_catalog_url or env_config.catalog_url,
92	        include_defaults=False if args.no_banodoco_defaults else env_config.include_defaults,
93	        include_mandatory=False if args.no_banodoco_mandatory else env_config.include_mandatory,
94	        cache_dir=Path(args.banodoco_cache_dir).expanduser() if args.banodoco_cache_dir else env_config.cache_dir,
95	        refresh=bool(args.banodoco_refresh or env_config.refresh),
96	        timeout_seconds=env_config.timeout_seconds,
97	    )

100	def _cmd_list(args: argparse.Namespace, registry: ExecutorRegistry) -> int:
101	    executors = registry.list(kind=args.kind)
102	    if args.json:
103	        print(json.dumps({"executors": [executor.to_dict() for executor in executors]}, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
104	        return 0
105	    for executor in executors:
106	        print(f"{executor.id}\t{executor.kind}\t{executor.name}")
107	    return 0
108	
109	
110	def _cmd_inspect(args: argparse.Namespace, registry: ExecutorRegistry) -> int:
111	    executor = registry.get(args.executor_id)
112	    if args.json:
113	        print(executor.to_json())
114	        return 0
115	    print(f"id: {executor.id}")
116	    print(f"name: {executor.name}")
117	    print(f"kind: {executor.kind}")
118	    print(f"version: {executor.version}")
119	    if executor.description:
120	        print(f"description: {executor.description}")
121	    _print_ports("inputs", executor.inputs)
122	    _print_outputs(executor)
123	    if executor.command is not None:
124	        print(f"command: {shlex.join(executor.command.argv)}")
125	    print(f"cache: {executor.cache.mode}")
126	    if executor.cache.sentinels:
127	        print(f"cache_sentinels: {', '.join(executor.cache.sentinels)}")
128	    if executor.isolation.binaries:
129	        print(f"binaries: {', '.join(executor.isolation.binaries)}")
130	    return 0
131	
132	
133	def _cmd_validate(args: argparse.Namespace, registry: ExecutorRegistry) -> int:
134	    registry.validate_all()
135	    executors = [registry.get(args.executor_id)] if args.executor_id else registry.list()
136	    missing_by_executor: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {}
137	    if args.check_binaries:
138	        from .runner import check_executor_binaries
139	
140	        for executor in executors:
141	            missing = check_executor_binaries(executor)
142	            if missing:
143	                missing_by_executor[executor.id] = missing
144	    if missing_by_executor:
145	        for executor_id, missing in missing_by_executor.items():
146	            print(f"{executor_id}: missing binaries: {', '.join(missing)}", file=sys.stderr)
147	        return 1
148	    if args.executor_id:
149	        print(f"{args.executor_id}: ok")
150	    else:
151	        print(f"{len(executors)} executor(s): ok")
152	    return 0
153	
154	
155	def _cmd_install(args: argparse.Namespace, registry: ExecutorRegistry) -> int:
156	    from .install import install_executor
157	
158	    executor = registry.get(args.executor_id)
159	    result = install_executor(executor, dry_run=bool(args.dry_run))
160	    plan = result.plan
161	    if plan.noop_reason:
162	        print(f"{executor.id}: no install needed: {plan.noop_reason}")
163	        return result.returncode
164	    if plan.environment_path is not None:
165	        print(f"env: {plan.environment_path}")
166	    if plan.python_path is not None:
167	        print(f"python: {plan.python_path}")
168	    for command in plan.commands:
169	        print(shlex.join(command))
170	    return result.returncode
171	
172	
173	def _cmd_run(args: argparse.Namespace, registry: ExecutorRegistry) -> int:
174	    from .runner import ExecutorRunRequest, run_executor
175	
176	    executor = registry.get(args.executor_id)
177	    if not args.out and _executor_needs_out(executor):
178	        raise ValueError("--out is required for this executor")
179	    request = ExecutorRunRequest(
180	        executor_id=args.executor_id,
181	        out=Path(args.out) if args.out else "",
182	        inputs=_run_inputs(args),
183	        brief=Path(args.brief) if args.brief else None,
184	        dry_run=bool(args.dry_run),
185	        check_binaries=bool(args.check_binaries),
186	        python_exec=args.python_exec,
187	        verbose=bool(args.verbose),
188	        thread=args.thread,
189	        variants=args.variants,
190	        from_ref=args.from_ref,
191	    )
192	    result = run_executor(request, registry)
193	    if result.missing_binaries:
194	        print(f"{args.executor_id}: missing binaries: {', '.join(result.missing_binaries)}", file=sys.stderr)
195	        return 1
196	    if result.skipped:
197	        print(f"{args.executor_id}: skipped: {result.skipped_reason}")
198	        return 0
199	    if result.command:
200	        print(shlex.join(result.command))
201	    if result.payload:
202	        print(json.dumps(dict(result.payload), separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True))
203	    return int(result.returncode or 0)
204	
205	
206	def _executor_needs_out(executor: ExecutorDefinition) -> bool:
207	    if executor.id == "upload.youtube":
208	        return False
209	    if executor.kind == "built_in" and "pipeline_step" in executor.metadata:
210	        return True
211	    if executor.command is not None:
212	        parts = [*executor.command.argv]
213	        if executor.command.cwd:
214	            parts.append(executor.command.cwd)
215	        parts.extend(executor.command.env.values())
216	        if any("{out}" in part for part in parts):
217	            return True
218	    return any((output.path_template and "{out}" in output.path_template) for output in executor.outputs)
219	
220	
221	def _run_inputs(args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict[str, Any]:
222	    inputs = _parse_input_values(args.input)
223	    for key in ("video_url", "title", "description", "privacy_status", "playlist_id"):
224	        value = getattr(args, key)
225	        if value not in (None, ""):
226	            inputs[key] = value
227	    tags = [*getattr(args, "tag", []), *getattr(args, "tags", [])]
228	    if tags:
229	        inputs["tags"] = tags
230	    if getattr(args, "made_for_kids", False):
231	        inputs["made_for_kids"] = True
232	    return inputs
233	
234	
235	def _parse_input_values(raw_values: list[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
236	    values: dict[str, str] = {}
237	    for raw in raw_values:
238	        if "=" not in raw:
239	            raise ValueError(f"invalid --input value {raw!r}; expected NAME=VALUE")
240	        key, value = raw.split("=", 1)
241	        key = key.strip()
242	        if not key:
243	            raise ValueError(f"invalid --input value {raw!r}; expected NAME=VALUE")
244	        values[key] = value
245	    return values
246	
247	
248	def _print_ports(label: str, ports: tuple[Any, ...]) -> None:
249	    if not ports:
250	        return
251	    print(f"{label}:")
252	    for port in ports:
253	        required = "required" if port.required else "optional"
254	        print(f"  - {port.name} ({port.type}, {required})")
255	
256	
257	def _print_outputs(executor: ExecutorDefinition) -> None:
258	    if not executor.outputs:
259	        return
260	    print("outputs:")
261	    for output in executor.outputs:
262	        placeholder = f", placeholder={output.placeholder}" if output.placeholder else ""
263	        print(f"  - {output.name} ({output.type}, {output.mode}{placeholder})")
264	
265	
266	if __name__ == "__main__":
267	    raise SystemExit(main())
268

2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import json
6	from importlib import resources

8	from types import MappingProxyType

10	
11	from artagents.executors.registry import ExecutorRegistry, load_default_registry as load_default_executor_registry
12	
13	from .schema import (
14	    OrchestratorDefinition,
15	    OrchestratorValidationError,
16	    load_orchestrator_manifest,
17	    validate_orchestrator_definition,
18	)
19	from .folder import load_folder_orchestrators

21	
22	BUNDLED_PACKAGE = "artagents.orchestrators.bundled"
23	CURATED_PACKAGE = "artagents.orchestrators.curated"
24	
25	
26	class OrchestratorRegistryError(OrchestratorValidationError):
27	    """Raised when an orchestrator registry is inconsistent."""
28	
29	
30	class OrchestratorRegistry:
31	    """Small in-memory registry keyed by orchestrator id."""
32	
33	    def __init__(
34	        self,
35	        orchestrators: Iterable[OrchestratorDefinition | dict[str, Any]] = (),
36	        *,
37	        executor_registry: ExecutorRegistry | None = None,
38	    ) -> None:
39	        self._orchestrators: dict[str, OrchestratorDefinition] = {}
40	        self._executor_registry = executor_registry
41	        for orchestrator in orchestrators:
42	            self.register(orchestrator)
43	
44	    def register(self, orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition | dict[str, Any]) -> OrchestratorDefinition:
45	        definition = validate_orchestrator_definition(orchestrator)
46	        if definition.id in self._orchestrators:
47	            raise OrchestratorRegistryError(f"duplicate orchestrator id {definition.id!r}")
48	        self._orchestrators[definition.id] = definition
49	        return definition
50	
51	    def get(self, orchestrator_id: str) -> OrchestratorDefinition:
52	        try:
53	            return self._orchestrators[orchestrator_id]
54	        except KeyError as exc:
55	            raise KeyError(f"unknown orchestrator id {orchestrator_id!r}") from exc
56	
57	    def list(self, kind: str | None = None) -> tuple[OrchestratorDefinition, ...]:
58	        if kind is not None and kind not in {"built_in", "external"}:
59	            raise OrchestratorRegistryError("kind must be one of ['built_in', 'external']")
60	        orchestrators: Iterable[OrchestratorDefinition] = self._orchestrators.values()
61	        if kind is not None:
62	            orchestrators = [orchestrator for orchestrator in orchestrators if orchestrator.kind == kind]
63	        return tuple(sorted(orchestrators, key=lambda orchestrator: orchestrator.id))
64	
65	    def validate_all(
66	        self,
67	        *,
68	        executor_registry: ExecutorRegistry | None = None,
69	    ) -> tuple[OrchestratorDefinition, ...]:
70	        for orchestrator in self._orchestrators.values():
71	            validate_orchestrator_definition(orchestrator)
72	        self._validate_child_executors(executor_registry=executor_registry)
73	        self._validate_child_orchestrators()
74	        return self.list()
75	
76	    def to_dict(self, kind: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
77	        return {"orchestrators": [orchestrator.to_dict() for orchestrator in self.list(kind=kind)]}
78	
79	    def to_json(self, *, kind: str | None = None, indent: int | None = 2) -> str:
80	        return json.dumps(self.to_dict(kind=kind), indent=indent, sort_keys=True)
81	
82	    def as_mapping(self) -> MappingProxyType[str, OrchestratorDefinition]:
83	        return MappingProxyType(dict(self._orchestrators))
84	
85	    def _validate_child_executors(self, *, executor_registry: ExecutorRegistry | None) -> None:
86	        registry = executor_registry or self._executor_registry or load_default_executor_registry()
87	        known_executor_ids = set(registry.as_mapping())
88	        for orchestrator in self._orchestrators.values():
89	            for child_executor in orchestrator.child_executors:
90	                if child_executor not in known_executor_ids:
91	                    raise OrchestratorRegistryError(
92	                        f"orchestrator {orchestrator.id!r} references unknown child executor {child_executor!r}"
93	                    )
94	
95	    def _validate_child_orchestrators(self) -> None:
96	        known_orchestrator_ids = set(self._orchestrators)
97	        graph: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {}
98	        for orchestrator in self._orchestrators.values():
99	            children: list[str] = []
100	            for child_orchestrator in orchestrator.child_orchestrators:
101	                if child_orchestrator not in known_orchestrator_ids:
102	                    raise OrchestratorRegistryError(
103	                        f"orchestrator {orchestrator.id!r} references unknown child orchestrator {child_orchestrator!r}"
104	                    )
105	                if child_orchestrator == orchestrator.id:
106	                    raise OrchestratorRegistryError(f"orchestrator {orchestrator.id!r} cannot reference itself")
107	                children.append(child_orchestrator)
108	            graph[orchestrator.id] = tuple(children)
109	        self._validate_no_cycles(graph)
110	
111	    def _validate_no_cycles(self, graph: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]) -> None:
112	        visiting: set[str] = set()
113	        visited: set[str] = set()
114	        stack: list[str] = []
115	
116	        def visit(orchestrator_id: str) -> None:
117	            if orchestrator_id in visited:
118	                return
119	            if orchestrator_id in visiting:
120	                cycle = stack[stack.index(orchestrator_id) :] + [orchestrator_id]
121	                raise OrchestratorRegistryError(f"orchestrator cycle detected: {' -> '.join(cycle)}")
122	            visiting.add(orchestrator_id)
123	            stack.append(orchestrator_id)
124	            for child in graph.get(orchestrator_id, ()):
125	                visit(child)
126	            stack.pop()
127	            visiting.remove(orchestrator_id)
128	            visited.add(orchestrator_id)
129	
130	        for orchestrator_id in sorted(graph):
131	            visit(orchestrator_id)
132	
133	
134	def load_builtin_orchestrators() -> tuple[OrchestratorDefinition, ...]:
135	    orchestrators: list[OrchestratorDefinition] = []
136	    for root in _builtin_folder_roots():
137	        orchestrators.extend(load_folder_orchestrators(root))
138	    return tuple(sorted(orchestrators, key=lambda o: o.id))
139	
140	
141	def load_curated_orchestrators() -> tuple[OrchestratorDefinition, ...]:
142	    orchestrators: list[OrchestratorDefinition] = []
143	    for path in _curated_manifest_paths():
144	        definition = load_orchestrator_manifest(path)
145	        if not _is_builtin_definition(definition):
146	            orchestrators.append(definition)
147	    for path in _curated_folder_roots():
148	        orchestrators.extend(definition for definition in load_folder_orchestrators(path) if not _is_builtin_definition(definition))
149	    return tuple(orchestrators)
150

Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive view. Let me compile my findings into a structured report:

## Framework Code Inventory: Executors, Orchestrators, Elements

### **EXECUTORS** (`artagents/executors/`)
Framework files and single-line descriptions:

1. **registry.py** (line 1): In-memory registry for executor definitions with built-in/bundled/curated/project loading
2. **schema.py** (line 1): Executor definition dataclass and validation; supports conditions, graph dependencies, pipeline requirements
3. **folder.py** (line 1): Folder-based executor discovery via subprocess metadata extraction
4. **runner.py** (line 1): Executor execution helpers; builds commands, evaluates conditions, runs with threading
5. **cli.py** (line 1): CLI with list/inspect/validate/install/run subcommands
6. **api.py** (line 1): ExecutorSpec code-first wrapper normalizing to ExecutorDefinition
7. **install.py** (line 1): Installation planning and execution; handles git-backed sources
8. **banodoco_catalog.py** (line 1): External catalog client for Banodoco website agent executors
9. **__init__.py** (line 1): Public API facade using lazy imports

**Exports**: ExecutorRegistry, ExecutorDefinition, ExecutorValidationError, ConditionSpec, GraphMetadata, ExecutorRunRequest, ExecutorRunResult, ExecutorSpec, ~25 functions

---

### **ORCHESTRATORS** (`artagents/orchestrators/`)
Framework files and single-line descriptions:

1. **registry.py** (line 1): In-memory registry for orchestrator definitions; validates child executors and orchestrators for cycles
2. **schema.py** (line 1): Orchestrator definition dataclass with RuntimeSpec; validates child refs (no cycle detection)
3. **folder.py** (line 1): Folder-based orchestrator discovery with subprocess extraction (mirrors executor folder.py)
4. **runner.py** (line 1): Orchestrator execution helpers; builds commands, imports Python runtimes, builds plans
5. **cli.py** (line 1): CLI with list/inspect/validate/run subcommands (similar to executors)
6. **api.py** (line 1): OrchestratorSpec code-first wrapper normalizing to OrchestratorDefinition
7. **__init__.py** (line 1): Public API facade using lazy imports

**Exports**: OrchestratorRegistry, OrchestratorDefinition, OrchestratorValidationError, RuntimeSpec, OrchestratorRunRequest, OrchestratorRunResult, OrchestratorSpec, ~12 functions

---

### **ELEMENTS** (`artagents/elements/`)
Framework files and single-line descriptions:

1. **registry.py** (line 1): Resolved registry by kind+id with source precedence (theme > overrides > managed > bundled); includes fork/override support
2. **schema.py** (line 1): Element definition with component/schema/defaults/metadata; supports effects/animations/transitions
3. **install.py** (line 1): Install planning for element dependencies (JS packages, Python requirements)
4. **cli.py** (line 1): CLI with list/inspect/validate/sync/fork/install subcommands
5. **catalog.py** (line 1): Helper facade mapping element kinds to workspace directories
6. **__init__.py** (line 1): Direct exports (not lazy like executors/orchestrators)

**Exports**: ElementRegistry, ElementDefinition, ElementValidationError, ElementSource, ElementConflict, ElementInstallPlan, ~8 functions

---

### **Near-Duplicates & Collapse Candidates**

| File | Executors | Orchestrators | Elements | Similarity |
|------|-----------|---------------|----------|-----------|
| **registry.py** | ExecutorRegistry(id→defn) | OrchestratorRegistry(id→defn, validates children) | ElementRegistry(kind+id→defn, precedence) | **60% similar**—all have register/get/list/to_json/as_mapping; orchestrators adds child validation; elements adds conflict tracking & fork support. Core pattern collapses to generic Registry<K,V>. |
| **schema.py** | ExecutorDefinition + ConditionSpec, GraphMetadata | OrchestratorDefinition + RuntimeSpec | ElementDefinition + ElementDependencies | **30% similar**—different domain models (executors are stateless; orchestrators compose executors; elements are theming). No practical unification. |
| **folder.py** | load_folder_executors (subprocess extraction) | load_folder_orchestrators (subprocess extraction) | (none) | **90% similar**—same discovery pattern, only manifest filename differs. _RESULT_PREFIX, subprocess call, error handling all identical. Collapse to generic folder loader. |
| **runner.py** | ExecutorRunRequest, execution logic | OrchestratorRunRequest, plan building, Python runtime import | (none) | **40% similar**—both parse placeholders, build commands. Executors are simpler; orchestrators compose child steps. Shared utils but separate concerns. |
| **cli.py** | list/inspect/validate/install/run | list/inspect/validate/run | list/inspect/validate/sync/fork/install | **50% similar**—CLI pattern repeatable (subparsers, registry loading, error handling), but subcommands differ per domain. Could share argument parsing utilities. |
| **api.py** | ExecutorSpec → ExecutorDefinition | OrchestratorSpec → OrchestratorDefinition | (none) | **85% similar**—both normalize code-first builders to definitions. Same pattern, different dataclasses. Collapse to generic Spec<T> wrapper. |
| **install.py** | Git-backed source planning + venv logic | (none) | Element dependency planning (npm/pip) | **15% similar**—executors are git/subprocess; elements are package managers. Different domains. |
| **__init__.py** | Lazy-load facade | Lazy-load facade | Direct exports | **70% similar** (executors/orchestrators only)—facade pattern repeatable. Elements uses a different strategy. |
| **banodoco_catalog.py** | External executor catalog | (none) | (none) | **Domain-specific**—no collapse. |
| **catalog.py** | (none) | (none) | Helper for workspace mapping | **Domain-specific**—no collapse. |

---

### **Unique/Non-Generalizing**

- **orchestrators/schema.py**: RuntimeSpec (python/command/module/function) is orchestrator-specific composition model.
- **orchestrators/runner.py**: importlib dynamic module loading for python runtimes—doesn't apply to executors or elements.
- **elements/registry.py**: Source precedence system, ElementConflict, fork/override logic, theme resolution—specific to theming.
- **executors/install.py**: Git source & manifest fetching—specific to external executor distribution.
- **executors/banodoco_catalog.py**: Web catalog client—executor distribution channel.
- **elements/install.py**: npm/pip dependency logic—specific to element build systems.

---

### **High-Value Collapse Targets**

1. **registry.py**: Core registry pattern (register, get, list, to_json, as_mapping) → `GenericRegistry<K, V>`
2. **folder.py**: Discovery & subprocess extraction → `GenericFolderLoader` (parameterized by manifest filename)
3. **api.py**: Code-first spec builders → `SpecBuilder<T>` wrapper
4. **cli.py**: Argument parsing & error handling skeleton → shared CLI utilities

**Complexity estimate**: registry & folder.py collapse would absorb 40-50% of framework code duplication. Schema, runner, api remain ~70% domain-specific.

77	
78	_PLACEHOLDER_RE = re.compile(r"\{([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)\}")
79	
80	
81	class ExecutorValidationError(ValueError):
82	    """Raised when a executor manifest or definition is structurally invalid."""
83	
84	
85	@dataclass(frozen=True)
86	class ConditionSpec:
87	    kind: str
88	    input: str | None = None
89	    path: str | None = None
90	    value: Any = None
91	
92	
93	@dataclass(frozen=True)
94	class GraphMetadata:
95	    depends_on: tuple[str, ...] = ()
96	    provides: tuple[str, ...] = ()
97	    consumes: tuple[str, ...] = ()
98	
99	
100	@dataclass(frozen=True)
101	class ExecutorDefinition:
102	    id: str
103	    name: str
104	    kind: str
105	    version: str
106	    description: str = ""
107	    inputs: tuple[ExecutorPort, ...] = ()
108	    outputs: tuple[ExecutorOutput, ...] = ()
109	    command: CommandSpec | None = None
110	    cache: CachePolicy = field(default_factory=CachePolicy)
111	    conditions: tuple[ConditionSpec, ...] = ()
112	    graph: GraphMetadata = field(default_factory=GraphMetadata)
113	    clip_kinds_supported: tuple[str, ...] = ()
114	    pipeline_requirements: tuple[str, ...] = ()
115	    isolation: IsolationMetadata = field(default_factory=IsolationMetadata)
116	    metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
117	
118	    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
119	        return _drop_none(asdict(self))
120	
121	    def to_json(self, *, indent: int | None = 2) -> str:
122	        return json.dumps(self.to_dict(), indent=indent, sort_keys=True)
123	
124	
125	def validate_executor_definition(raw: Any) -> ExecutorDefinition:
126	    if isinstance(raw, ExecutorDefinition):
127	        executor = raw
128	    else:
129	        executor = _parse_executor(raw)
130	    _validate_executor(executor)
131	    return executor
132	
133	
134	def load_executor_manifest(path: str | Path) -> ExecutorDefinition:
135	    definitions = load_executor_manifest_definitions(path)
136	    if len(definitions) != 1:
137	        raise ExecutorValidationError(f"executor manifest must define exactly one executor: {Path(path)}")
138	    return definitions[0]
139	
140	
141	def load_executor_manifest_definitions(path: str | Path) -> tuple[ExecutorDefinition, ...]:
142	    manifest_path = Path(path)
143	    try:
144	        raw = _load_manifest_payload(manifest_path)
145	    except FileNotFoundError as exc:
146	        raise ExecutorValidationError(f"executor manifest not found: {manifest_path}") from exc
147	    except ValueError as exc:
148	        raise ExecutorValidationError(f"invalid executor manifest {manifest_path}: {exc}") from exc
149	    try:
150	        return _validate_manifest_payload(raw)

14	class ElementInstallTest(unittest.TestCase):
15	    def element_with_dependencies(self):
16	        element = load_default_registry().get("effects", "text-card")
17	        return type(element)(
18	            **{
19	                **element.__dict__,
20	                "metadata": {
21	                    **element.metadata,
22	                    "dependencies": {
23	                        "js_packages": ["@example/element-widget@1.2.3"],
24	                        "python_requirements": ["example-element==4.5.6"],
25	                    },
26	                },
27	                "dependencies": type(element.dependencies)(
28	                    js_packages=("@example/element-widget@1.2.3",),
29	                    python_requirements=("example-element==4.5.6",),
30	                ),
31	            }
32	        )
33	
34	    def test_element_install_plan_uses_only_element_local_paths(self) -> None:
35	        element = self.element_with_dependencies()
36	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
37	            project_root = Path(tmp)
38	            plan = build_element_install_plan(element, project_root=project_root)
39	
40	        install_root = project_root / ".artagents" / "elements" / "effects-text-card"
41	        venv = install_root / "venv"
42	        node = install_root / "node"
43	        python = venv / "bin" / "python"
44	
45	        self.assertEqual(plan.root, install_root)
46	        self.assertEqual(plan.venv_path, venv)
47	        self.assertEqual(plan.node_prefix, node)
48	        self.assertEqual(
49	            plan.commands,
50	            (
51	                ("uv", "venv", str(venv)),
52	                ("uv", "pip", "install", "--python", str(python), "example-element==4.5.6"),
53	                ("npm", "install", "--prefix", str(node), "@example/element-widget@1.2.3"),
54	            ),
55	        )
56	        self.assertNotIn("pip install example-element", "\n".join(plan.command_lines()))
57	        self.assertNotIn("npm install @example/element-widget", "\n".join(plan.command_lines()))
58	
59	    def test_element_install_dry_run_does_not_create_dirs_or_run_package_managers(self) -> None:
60	        element = self.element_with_dependencies()
61	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
62	            project_root = Path(tmp)
63	            with mock.patch("artagents.elements.install.subprocess.run") as run:
64	                result = install_element(element, project_root=project_root, dry_run=True)
65	
66	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0)
67	            self.assertFalse(result.plan.root.exists())
68	            run.assert_not_called()
69	
70	    def test_element_install_apply_runs_explicit_local_commands(self) -> None:
71	        element = self.element_with_dependencies()
72	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
73	            project_root = Path(tmp)
74	            completed = mock.Mock(returncode=0)
75	            with mock.patch("artagents.elements.install.subprocess.run", return_value=completed) as run:
76	                result = install_element(element, project_root=project_root, dry_run=False)
77	
78	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0)
79	            self.assertTrue(result.plan.root.is_dir())
80	            self.assertEqual([call.args[0] for call in run.call_args_list], list(result.plan.commands))
81	
82	    def test_executor_install_paths_use_executor_cache_root_for_vibecomfy_and_moirae(self) -> None:
83	        registry = load_executor_registry()
84	        vibe_run = registry.get("external.vibecomfy.run")
85	        vibe_validate = registry.get("external.vibecomfy.validate")
86	        moirae = registry.get("external.moirae")
87	
88	        self.assertEqual(executor_environment_path(vibe_run), executor_environment_path(vibe_validate))
89	        self.assertEqual(executor_python_path(vibe_run), executor_python_path(vibe_validate))
90	        self.assertTrue(str(executor_environment_path(vibe_run)).endswith(".artagents/executors/vibecomfy/venv"))
91	        self.assertTrue(str(executor_environment_path(moirae)).endswith(".artagents/executors/external.moirae/venv"))
92	
93	        vibe_plan = build_executor_install_plan(vibe_run)
94	        moirae_plan = build_executor_install_plan(moirae)
95	        self.assertEqual(vibe_plan.environment_path, executor_environment_path(vibe_run))
96	        self.assertEqual(moirae_plan.environment_path, executor_environment_path(moirae))
97	        self.assertIn("-r", vibe_plan.commands[1])
98	        self.assertIn("-r", moirae_plan.commands[1])

12	class OnboardingDocsTest(unittest.TestCase):
13	    def read_docs(self) -> str:
14	        return "\n".join(
15	            (ROOT / path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
16	            for path in (
17	                "README.md",
18	                "AGENTS.md",
19	                "SKILL.md",
20	                "docs/architecture.md",
21	                "docs/creating-tools.md",
22	            )
23	        )
24	
25	    def run_artagents(self, *args: str) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
26	        return subprocess.run(
27	            [sys.executable, "-m", "artagents", *args],
28	            cwd=ROOT,
29	            text=True,
30	            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
31	            stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
32	            check=False,
33	        )
34	
35	    def test_onboarding_docs_name_canonical_terms_and_guardrails(self) -> None:
36	        text = self.read_docs()
37	        required = (
38	            "orchestrators",
39	            "executors",
40	            "elements",
41	            "git status --short",
42	            "python3 -m artagents doctor",
43	            "python3 -m artagents --help",
44	            "python3 -m artagents",
45	            "executable package gateway",
46	            "python3 -m artagents setup",
47	            ".artagents/elements/overrides",
48	            "python3 scripts/gen_effect_registry.py",
49	            "artagents/executors/moirae/STAGE.md",
50	            "artagents/executors/vibecomfy/STAGE.md",
51	            "artagents/orchestrators/<slug>/{orchestrator.yaml,STAGE.md,run.py}",
52	            "artagents/executors/<slug>/{executor.yaml,STAGE.md,run.py}",
53	            "Top-level `artagents/*.py`",
54	            "examples/briefs/",

56	            "docs/templates/executor/",
57	            "docs/templates/orchestrator/",
58	            "docs/templates/element/",

66	        for phrase in required:

## Content Inventory for Migration to `artagents/packs/builtin/`

**COUNTS:** 33 executors, 4 orchestrators, 9 elements (46 total things)

### EXECUTORS (33)
All have standard structure: `executor.yaml`, `__init__.py`, `run.py`, `STAGE.md`, `__pycache__/`

**With isolated dependencies:**
- `moirae` — has `requirements.txt`
- `vibecomfy` — has `requirements.txt`

**With src/ subdirectories:**
- `refine` — contains `/src`
- `upload_youtube` — contains `/src`

**Standard (28):** arrange, asset_cache, audio_understand, boundary_candidates, cut, editor_review, generate_image, human_notes, inspect_cut, iteration_assemble, iteration_prepare, open_in_reigh, pool_build, pool_merge, publish, quality_zones, quote_scout, reigh_data, render, scene_describe, scenes, shots, sprite_sheet, transcribe, triage, understand, validate, video_understand, visual_understand

### ORCHESTRATORS (4)
All standard: `orchestrator.yaml`, `__init__.py`, `run.py`, `STAGE.md`, `__pycache__/`

**All standard:** event_talks, hype, logo_ideas, thumbnail_maker

### ELEMENTS (9)
Organized under three categories (animations, effects, transitions). All use `.json` manifests (`meta.json`, `schema.json`, `defaults.json`, `component.tsx`), not YAML.

**Animations (6):** fade, fade-up, scale-in, slide-left, slide-up, type-on
**Effects (1):** text-card
**Transitions (2):** cross-fade, fade

### NOTES
- **No non-standard manifests:** All executors/orchestrators follow naming convention (executor.yaml, orchestrator.yaml). Elements use JSON instead of YAML.
- **No external registrations:** No registered things found outside the three target directories.
- **No submodule imports detected:** `refine` and `upload_youtube` have `src/` but appear isolated; `moirae` and `vibecomfy` have requirements isolation.
- **bundled/ directories:** `artagents/executors/bundled/` and `artagents/orchestrators/bundled/` are empty (only `__init__.py` + `__pycache__`), so no collision risk.

**Mechanical effort sizing:** Straightforward moves for 46 folders. Primary complexity: preserve the 4 isolated-dependency folders and 2 with `src/` subdirs as-is.

SKILL.md:Public folders have exactly one format: `artagents/orchestrators/<slug>/{orchestrator.yaml,STAGE.md,run.py}` and `artagents/executors/<slug>/{executor.yaml,STAGE.md,run.py}`, with optional local `src/` modules. Top-level `artagents/*.py` files are shared libraries or system commands, not alternate runnable implementations.
SKILL.md:Element source priority: active theme → `.artagents/elements/overrides` → `.artagents/elements/managed` → `artagents/elements/bundled`.
SKILL.md:- Treat curated tool stages as protected unless explicitly asked to edit them — notably `artagents/executors/moirae/STAGE.md` and `artagents/executors/vibecomfy/STAGE.md`.
docs/architecture.md:Default orchestrators include `builtin.hype`, `builtin.event_talks`, and `builtin.thumbnail_maker`. Default executors include every `STEP_ORDER` built-in, upload/action executors, `builtin.understand` (audio/visual/video dispatcher), `builtin.generate_image` (with a `saint-peter-of-banodoco` onboarding preset), Moirae, and VibeComfy. Default elements include bundled effects, animations, and transitions that can be synced into `.artagents/elements/managed` and forked into `.artagents/elements/overrides`.

docs/architecture.md:| `artagents/orchestrators/hype` | Orchestrator | Canonical built-in hype orchestrator folder. |
docs/architecture.md:| `artagents/orchestrators/event_talks` | Orchestrator | Canonical event-talk discovery and rendering workflow folder. |
docs/architecture.md:| `artagents/orchestrators/thumbnail_maker` | Orchestrator | Canonical source-evidence thumbnail workflow folder. |
docs/architecture.md:| `artagents/orchestrators/*` | Orchestrator canonical package | Folderized orchestrator manifests, registry, runner, and CLI. |
docs/architecture.md:Every runnable tool is a built-in or external executor exposed from exactly one canonical folder under `artagents/executors/<slug>/`.
docs/architecture.md:| Hype pipeline stages | `artagents/executors/{transcribe,scenes,quality_zones,shots,triage,scene_describe,quote_scout,pool_build,pool_merge,arrange,cut,refine,render,editor_review,validate}` | `STEP_ORDER` stages used by the hype orchestrator. |
docs/architecture.md:| Understanding tools | `artagents/executors/{audio_understand,visual_understand,video_understand,understand}` | Concrete media understanding tools, plus a thin `understand` dispatcher executor that selects modality via `--mode`. |
docs/architecture.md:| Standalone/service tools | `artagents/executors/{asset_cache,boundary_candidates,generate_image,human_notes,inspect_cut,open_in_reigh,publish,reigh_data,sprite_sheet,upload_youtube}` | Standalone executor capabilities. |
docs/architecture.md:| External tools | `artagents/executors/{vibecomfy,moirae}` | VibeComfy and Moirae are external executors only, not orchestrators. |
docs/architecture.md:| `artagents/elements/schema.py` | Element support | Element schema and dependency metadata. |
docs/architecture.md:| `artagents/elements/registry.py` | Element support | Deterministic resolution: active theme, overrides, managed, bundled. |
docs/architecture.md:| `artagents/elements/bundled/{effects,animations,transitions}` | Element support | Bundled default managed elements. |
docs/architecture.md:| `.artagents/elements/overrides` | Element support | User-editable fork target for defaults and custom elements. |
docs/architecture.md:| `.artagents/elements/managed` | Element support | Installed managed elements that should not be overwritten by user overrides. |
docs/architecture.md:| `artagents/elements/catalog.py` | Element support | Effect, animation, and transition catalog support used by render validation. |
docs/architecture.md:| `artagents/executors/refine/src/reviewers/*` | Executor-owned library | Focused review heuristics used only by the refine executor. |
docs/architecture.md:| `artagents/executors/upload_youtube/src/social_publish.py` | Executor-owned library | Social publishing client logic used by `upload.youtube`. |

docs/design-thread-layer.md:- The existing chokepoints are `run_executor()` in `artagents/executors/runner.py:75` and the orchestrator dispatch in `artagents/orchestrators/runner.py`. All new behavior lives in or above these wrappers — **no executor or orchestrator implementation is modified except where it produces variant outputs.**
docs/design-thread-layer.md:- **SD-030** — Single chokepoint integration: `run_executor()` at `artagents/executors/runner.py:75` and the orchestrator-runner equivalent are wrapped with `threads.begin(request)` / `threads.finalize(record_id, result)`. The wrapper is a no-op when: `dry_run=True`, `request.out` is unwritable or under `tempfile.gettempdir()`, `request.thread == "@none"`, or env `ARTAGENTS_THREADS_OFF=1` is set. **No executor's `run.py` is modified except where it produces variant outputs.** _load_bearing: true_
docs/design-thread-layer.md:  - `artagents/executors/generate_image/run.py` — declare `role: variant` on the N images; emit `group` from `(run_id, prompt_index)`; populate `preview_modes` and `duration`.
docs/design-thread-layer.md:  - `artagents/orchestrators/logo_ideas/run.py` — fold existing rich per-candidate metadata (`name`, `rationale`, `prompt`, `generated.*`) into `variant_meta` and stamp `role: variant`, `group`, `group_index`.
docs/sprint-thread-layer.md:- **Fail-closed reminder**: the default output artifact role is `other` in v1; only `artagents/executors/generate_image/run.py` and `artagents/orchestrators/logo_ideas/run.py` opt into `role: variant` per SD-031 and DEF-4. No other executor `run.py` is modified.
docs/sprint-thread-layer.md:- Modify `ExecutorRunRequest` at `artagents/executors/runner.py:43` and the orchestrator run request equivalent to carry `thread`, `variants`, and `from_variant`.
docs/sprint-thread-layer.md:- Wrap `run_executor()` at `artagents/executors/runner.py:75` and the orchestrator-runner equivalent with begin/finalize.
docs/sprint-thread-layer.md:- Leave the `upload.youtube` short-circuit at `artagents/executors/runner.py:78` untouched; verify it produces zero thread artifacts and zero errors.
docs/sprint-thread-layer.md:- Modify `_run_external_executor` at `artagents/executors/runner.py:258` to thread `ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID` into the subprocess env when present, so external executor processes inherit the parent thread without re-stamping.

## Install/Venv Lifecycle Mapping

### 1. **Venv Location & Safe-ID Derivation**

**Location:** `.artagents/executors/<safe-id>/venv` relative to repo root
- File: `/artagents/executors/install.py:56` → `executor_environment_path()`
- Python executable: `.artagents/executors/<safe-id>/venv/bin/python` (or `Scripts/python.exe` on Windows)

**Safe-ID Logic:** `/artagents/executors/install.py:205-213`
- Primary: Uses `executor.metadata['package_id']` if present (allows grouping multiple executors under one venv, e.g., vibecomfy.run + vibecomfy.validate → `vibecomfy` venv)
- Fallback: Uses `executor.id` 
- Sanitization: `_safe_executor_id()` converts non-alphanumeric chars to underscores: `re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]+", "_", executor_id)`
- Test confirms: `vibecomfy.run` and `vibecomfy.validate` both resolve to `.artagents/executors/vibecomfy/venv` via `package_id` metadata

### 2. **Dependency Resolution Order**

**Per `/artagents/executors/install.py:183-195` (`_dependency_install_command()`)**
1. **executor.metadata['pyproject_file']** → `uv pip install <pyproject_dir>` (metadata path resolved via `_metadata_path()`)
2. **executor.metadata['requirements_file']** → `uv pip install -r <requirements.txt>`
3. **executor.isolation.requirements** → `uv pip install <pkg1> <pkg2> ...` (inline from executor.yaml)
4. **None** → no-op (built-in executors skip install entirely)

No pack-level pyproject.toml concept exists yet. Each executor's dependencies are independent.

### 3. **Install Command Invocation**

**Per-executor only, no bulk mode:**
- `/artagents/executors/cli.py:155-170` (`_cmd_install()`) → single executor ID arg
- `/artagents/executors/install.py:97-107` (`install_executor()`) → processes one executor at a time via `subprocess.run()`
- Two sequential commands per install: `uv venv <path>` then `uv pip install ...`

### 4. **.artagents/ Structure & Gitignore**

**Location:** `.artagents/` at repo root (REPO_ROOT, computed from install.py's `__file__`)

**Structure:**
```
.artagents/
├── executors/
│   ├── <safe-id>/venv/          (executor venvs)
│   ├── <safe-id>/venv/bin/python
│   └── banodoco-executors/      (git-cached executor manifests)
├── elements/
│   ├── <kind>-<id>/venv/        (element Python venvs)
│   ├── <kind>-<id>/node/        (element npm installs)
│   ├── managed/                 (auto-installed elements)
│   └── overrides/               (user-customized elements)
├── threads.json                 (run provenance index)
└── iteration_cache/             (intermediate build artifacts)
```

**Gitignored:** Yes, `.artagents/` explicitly listed in `.gitignore` (line 46)

### 5. **Cached Install-State Files**

**No lockfiles tied to executor paths.** Found:
- `uv` doesn't write lockfiles to `.artagents/executors/<id>/` (no uv.lock)
- Git manifest sources cache hashes in path: `/artagents/executors/install.py:227-230` uses `sha256(repo_url, ref, manifest_path)[:16]` as a digest in `.artagents/banodoco-executors/`
- Elements use `threads.json` for run provenance, not install state
- No per-executor manifest.json or hash files for pinning

**Impact:** Install state is ephemeral; venv created fresh each install cycle.

### 6. **Runtime Venv Resolution**

**Path lookup + conditional error:**
- `/artagents/executors/runner.py:347-363` (`_resolve_python_exec()`)
  - If executor uses `{python_exec}` placeholder AND kind==external AND isolation.mode==subprocess:
    - Calls `executor_python_path(executor)` 
    - `.is_file()` check → if missing, raises error: *"requires an installed Python environment; run `python3 -m artagents executors install`"*
  - Otherwise falls back to `sys.executable`
- **No fallback to system python if venv missing**; explicit install required

### 7. **Pack-Level Shared Venv Feasibility**

**Current mechanism for sharing already works via metadata:**
- `executor.metadata['package_id']` already groups multiple executors to one venv (vibecomfy.run + vibecomfy.validate → vibecomfy)
- To extend to packs: rename `package_id` → `venv_group_id` or add `pack` field, reuse `_safe_executor_id()` logic

**Invasive changes:**
- **Moderate impact** — changes confined to `/artagents/executors/install.py` (4 functions: `executor_environment_path`, `executor_python_path`, `_executor_install_id`, `_safe_executor_id`)
- `/artagents/executors/runner.py` unchanged (already calls `executor_python_path()` indirectly)
- `/artagents/executors/cli.py` unchanged (install still per-executor command)
- **No changes needed** in element install logic (elements use different `.artagents/elements/` tree)

**Pack-level pyproject.toml:** Would require new logic to enumerate pack members and merge deps before install.

### 8. **Migration Path for Existing Installs**

**Silent reinstall needed.**

Current: `.artagents/executors/<bare-name>/venv`  
New: `.artagents/executors/<pack>.<bare-name>/venv`

**Issues:**
- Path is hardcoded in `/artagents/executors/install.py:56` via `executor_environment_path()`
- Old venvs at `.artagents/executors/vibecomfy/venv` become orphaned if ID changes to `core.vibecomfy`
- Runtime `.is_file()` check will fail, user sees "requires install" error

**Recommended strategy:**
1. Add migration helper to symlink old path → new path (or copy venv)
2. Or: keep backward-compatibility fallback in `executor_environment_path()` (check old path if new path missing)
3. Or: one-time CLI command: `python3 -m artagents executors migrate-venvs --pack <name>`

**Test coverage:** `/tests/test_elements_install.py:82-99` validates path structure; new tests should verify pack prefix handling.

---

**Summary:** Install is minimal, single-pass, per-executor. Pack introduction is low-risk (localized to install.py's path logic), but migration requires explicit user action or compatibility shim.

## Migration Impact Report: "Packs" ID Qualification (`<pack>.<name>`)

### 1. **CLI Subcommands Surface**

The CLI is already structured to accept executor/orchestrator ids as positional arguments:

**Executors CLI** (`artagents/executors/cli.py:17-83`):
- `list` - Lists all executors; no id needed
- `inspect <executor_id>` - Inspects one executor (line 48)
- `validate [<executor_id>]` - Validates all or one executor (line 53)
- `install <executor_id>` - Installs dependencies for one executor (line 58)
- `run <executor_id>` - Runs one executor (line 63)

**Orchestrators CLI** (`artagents/orchestrators/cli.py:32-72`):
- `list` - Lists all orchestrators; no id needed
- `inspect <orchestrator_id>` - Inspects one orchestrator (line 52)
- `validate [<orchestrator_id>]` - Validates all or one orchestrator (line 57)
- `run <orchestrator_id>` - Runs one orchestrator (line 61)

**Elements CLI** (`artagents/elements/cli.py:30-73`):
- `list` - Lists all elements; no id needed
- `inspect <kind> <element_id>` - Inspects one element (lines 44-45)
- `validate [<kind>] [<element_id>]` - Validates elements (lines 50-51)
- `fork <kind> <element_id>` - Forks an element (lines 59-60)
- `install <kind> <element_id>` - Installs one element (lines 65-66)

Note: **Elements already use two-part ids** (`kind/id`), providing a template for packs.

**Pipeline gateway** (`artagents/pipeline.py:66-100`):
- Dispatches to subcommand modules via `artagents {executors,orchestrators,elements,thread} <subcommand>`

### 2. **How IDs Are Referenced Today**

**User-facing id references:**
- CLI accepts bare executor ids (`cut`, `hype`) and the system auto-prefixes them as `builtin.<name>` (see `artagents/executors/registry.py:96` and `artagents/orchestrators/hype/run.py:210`)
- No aliasing layer exists—pure string lookups via `registry.get(executor_id)` and `registry.get(orchestrator_id)`

**Internal string references:**
- `child_executors` and `child_orchestrators` fields in YAML manifests already use **qualified ids**: `artagents/orchestrators/hype/orchestrator.yaml:5-21` lists `["builtin.transcribe", "builtin.cut", ...]`
- Executor dependency graphs store qualified ids: `artagents/executors/render/executor.yaml:21-34` has `"depends_on": ["builtin.transcribe", ..., "builtin.cut"]`
- Test files reference qualified ids: `tests/test_canonical_cli.py:26,44,57,61,65` use `"builtin.hype"` and `"builtin.render"`

**File-based config:**
- Executor/orchestrator YAML metadata is loaded dynamically; folder structure under `artagents/executors/{builtin,bundled,curated}` determines kind—ids are not derived from paths

### 3. **What Would Break with `cut` → `builtin.cut`**

The system **already uses qualified ids internally**. The breaking change is the **user-facing bare-name interface**:

- Users currently run `ag executors run cut` but would need `ag executors run builtin.cut` (already required for some external executors)
- CLI help text shows bare `cut` (line 106, `artagents/executors/cli.py`) but actually stores `builtin.cut` in manifests
- Tests hardcode `"builtin.hype"` and `"builtin.render"` (lines 26, 44, 57, 61 in `test_canonical_cli.py`)—these would still work
- Test step names like `"cut"`, `"hype"` (line 18 in `test_pipeline_caching.py`) refer to step/orchestrator names, not executor ids; they're separate from id qualification

**Lines that would require migration:**

| File | Line | Current | Impact |
|------|------|---------|--------|
| `artagents/executors/cli.py` | 58, 63, 111 | `args.executor_id` args | CLI accepts new qualified format |
| `artagents/orchestrators/cli.py` | 52, 61, 100 | `args.orchestrator_id` args | CLI accepts new qualified format |
| `artagents/setup_cli.py` | 46, 53 | Element kind/id output | Already two-part; exemplar for packs |
| `artagents/orchestrators/registry.py` | 87, 89-93 | Child executor validation | Validates qualified ids already |
| `artagents/executors/registry.py` | 56-60, 86-92 | Registry lookups | Pure string match; id format transparent |

### 4. **Aliasing / Rewriting Layer**

**None currently exists for ids.** The system does have one legacy alias pattern:
- `artagents/orchestrators/hype/run.py:210` constructs `executor_id = f"builtin.{name}"` for built-in steps
- `artagents/executors/understand/run.py:176` has a `_resolve_alias()` function, but it's for internal mode selection, not id aliasing

The migration will require a lookup table if bare-name backwards compatibility is needed. Currently, the code assumes callers already know to use `builtin.cut`, not `cut`.

### 5. **Install Command Semantics**

`ag executors install <executor_id>` works per-executor:
- Accepts an executor id as a positional arg (line 58, `artagents/executors/cli.py`)
- Calls `install_executor(executor, dry_run=bool(args.dry_run))` (line 159, `artagents/executors/cli.py`)
- No bulk install flag; separate invocation per executor

Install plan building is transparent to id format—it uses the `executor` object, not the string id (line 100, `artagents/executors/install.py`).

---

**Invasiveness Summary:** The migration is **low-impact on user-facing surface**—the CLI already expects (or should expect) qualified ids. The main breakage is in manifests and internal references, which already use `builtin.` qualification. Adding a new pack prefix (e.g., `custom.`, `bundled.`) requires updating:
1. Executor/orchestrator YAML `id` fields
2. Child executor/orchestrator reference lists in orchestrator manifests
3. Dependency graphs in executor manifests
4. Test assertions (< 30 instances)
5. Optional: A compatibility layer for bare-name → qualified-name mapping if existing workflows must not break
